### [335] [The Ganglion Network Model: Evolving Trapped Phases in Porous Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39041)
*Yashar Mehmani*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"孔隙网络团簇模型(GNM)"，一种基于树图（ganglion network）的降阶方法，无需解方程组即可模拟多孔介质中部分混溶团簇群体的演化，并在2.5D/3D域中验证了其精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多孔介质中滞留的部分混溶团簇（跨越单个或多个孔隙、通过扩散传质演化）在CO₂/H₂地质封存与燃料电池等应用中普遍存在。传统孔隙网络模型仍需在孔隙尺度上求解方程组，成本随域尺寸增长，难以高效模拟大量团簇群体的演化。

Method: 从给定样品的孔隙尺度图像中提取树形图结构（ganglion network），图中每个节点编码一种可能的团簇构型，保留几何与拓扑复杂度。将每个团簇表示为图上的"粒子"并按一组规则追踪其演化，规则涵盖孔隙侵入、回缩、颈缩（snap-off）、破碎与合并等毛细事件。方法不需求解任何方程组，计算成本随团簇数量而非域尺寸缩放。

Result: 在2.5D与3D域中、针对经历熟化（ripening）、溶解与生长的团簇群体，将GNM与基于图像的孔隙网络模型进行对比验证，在团簇统计量、聚集性质与空间构型上均取得良好一致性。作者进一步论证ganglion network是从单孔扩展到多孔团簇的Ostwald熟化动力学理论所需的统计空间，并给出了扩展思路。

Conclusion: GNM为模拟多孔介质中滞留相的演化提供了高效且保真的降阶工具，其图结构可作为连接单孔与多孔Ostwald熟化动力学理论的统计空间，并有望推广至滞留相的其他动态过程建模。

Abstract: Partially miscible ganglia trapped within porous media, spanning one or multiple pores and evolving through diffusive mass transfer, are common in subsurface (e.g., CO$_2$ and H$_2$ storage) and manufacturing (e.g., fuel cells) applications. We present the ganglion network model (GNM), a reduced-order method for simulating how a population of such ganglia evolves inside an arbitrary porous microstructure. GNM operates on a tree graph, the ganglion network, extracted from the pore-scale image of a given sample. Each point on the graph encodes a possible ganglion configuration in the void space, without any loss of geometric or topological complexity. The evolution of a population is modeled by representing each ganglion as a particle on the graph and tracking it according to a set of rules formulated herein. The rules capture capillary events such as pore invasion, retraction, snap-off, fragmentation, and merger. Unlike pore-network models, another graph-based modeling tool at the pore scale, GNM solves no system of equations and its cost scales with ganglion count, not domain size. We validate GNM against an image-based pore-network model in 2.5D and 3D domains with populations undergoing ripening, dissolution, and growth. We find good agreement in ganglion statistics, aggregate properties, and spatial configuration. We further argue that the ganglion network is the statistical space needed for extending kinetic theories of Ostwald ripening from single- to multi-pore ganglia, and provide an outline for how to do this. GNM opens the door to modeling other dynamics of trapped phases in porous media.

</details>


### [336] [A Variance-Decomposition Formula for Direct and Adjoint Monte Carlo Particle Transport Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39210)
*Paul Rovel,Coline Larmier,Davide Mancusi,Andrea Zoia*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了蒙卡固定源粒子输运中方差的分解公式，将模拟方差表示为输运过程中"方差贡献"之和，并用于分析零方差方案与方差缩减技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定源蒙特卡洛粒子输运中，获得可接受的响应方差至关重要；而现有唯一的严格分析工具——矩方程框架——在实践中过于繁琐难用。

Method: 推导一个方差分解公式，将蒙特卡洛模拟的方差写成在整个粒子输运过程中累积的"方差贡献"之和；该公式同时适用于正向（direct）与伴随（adjoint）模拟。

Result: 该公式为定位方差产生机制、理解常见方差缩减技术乃至设计新技术提供了新工具；在零方差方案与若干现有方差缩减技术的分析中得到应用，明确其优缺点，并有数值算例验证理论结论。

Conclusion: 方差分解公式是分析蒙特卡洛方差的有效且实用的工具，可替代繁琐的矩方程框架，并为方差缩减研究提供系统性视角。

Abstract: In fixed-source Monte Carlo particle-transport problems, obtaining an acceptable variance on the sought response is of paramount importance. Currently, the only rigorous tool to analyze the variance of such games is the framework of the moment equations, which is unfortunately unwieldy to use in practice. In this paper, we establish a formula that enables expressing the variance of a Monte Carlo simulation as a sum of 'variance contributions' collected throughout the underlying particletransport process. This formula applies to both direct and adjoint games, and provides a new tool to pinpoint the variance-inducing mechanisms in Monte Carlo simulations, understand common variance-reduction techniques, and even conceive new ones. We showcase the use of the variance-decomposition formula on several applications. In particular, we revisit zero-variance schemes and analyze existing variance-reduction techniques, underlining their strengths and weaknesses. A few relevant numerical examples substantiate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [337] [MyTm: An Automated Melting Temperature Calculation Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39686)
*Y. S. Huang,H. X. Song,Y. Sun,J. L. Li,Y. L. Xu,F. C. Wu,Y. C. Gan,Y. F. Wang,H. Wang,Hua Y. Geng*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍 MyTm 工具包，基于分子动力学自动计算材料熔点，并引入机器学习分类固/液原子，解决传统分类精度低的问题，实现高通量自动化熔点计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熔点计算是计算材料科学的重要课题，在材料的高通量筛选与人工智能辅助设计中需快速、自主评估熔点。但传统分子动力学熔点模拟依赖大量繁琐的人工操作，难以支撑大规模熔点计算。

Method: 提出 MyTm 工具包，以分子动力学自动确定熔点：方法完全模块化，通过模块组合支持直接加热法、空穴法、改进空穴法、固液共存法和 Z 法等常用方案，实现全自动熔点计算；同时提出并采用机器学习方法识别与分类类固态和类液态原子，以替代传统分类方式。

Result: 机器学习分类有效缓解了传统分类方法精度偏低的问题，使自动化高通量熔点计算流程成为可能；MyTm 的鲁棒性与有效性已在若干被充分研究的体系中得到验证。

Conclusion: MyTm 通过模块化分子动力学方法与机器学习原子分类相结合，实现了鲁棒、有效的自动化熔点计算，为高通量材料筛选与 AI 辅助设计提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Melting temperature calculation is one of the important topics in computational materials science. In high-throughput in silico screening and artificial intelligence assisted design of materials, it usually requires a rapid and autonomous assessment of the melting temperature of the target. Unfortunately, molecular dynamics (MD) simulations of the melting point require many cumbersome and manual operations, making large-scale calculation of the melting point challenging. In this work, we introduce MyTm, a toolkit that employs MD to automatically determine the melting point. The method is fully modularized, and by combining these modules, the program enables fully automated melting calculations by using commonly adopted approaches, including the direct-heating method, the void method, the modified void method, the solid-liquid coexistence method, and the Z method. Moreover, a machine learning (ML) method is proposed and employed to recognize and classify the solid like and liquid like atoms, which effectively resolve the low accuracy issue in conventional classification approaches, thus making the automated high throughput pipeline of melting-point calculation possible. The robustness and efficacy of MyTm have been demonstrated by several well studied systems.

</details>

### [338] [Reference-Measure Freedom of the Effective Hamiltonian: From Stochastic Thermodynamics to the Macroscopic Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38994)
*Ryuichi Okamoto*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 有效哈密顿量（EH）在坐标变换下不具标量性所引发的\"不良定义\"争议，实源于参考测度（规范）的隐式改变。本文在慢变量流形上内蕴地建立平衡测度与Fokker–Planck方程，证明EH是相对于所选规范的标量函数，不同EH（含常规EH与扩散依赖EH）只是同一内蕴平衡测度与随机动力学的不同规范表示；规范选择具有可操作意义，且在大偏差极限下规范依赖项成为次领头项，领头EH由与规范无关的大偏差速率函数决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效哈密顿量（亦称平均力势或自由能景观）广泛用于描述化学与软物质系统的平衡性质和慢动力学，但常规EH被指在坐标变换下不按标量变换，因而\"定义不良\"。该问题长期困扰理论界，亟需厘清其根源并给出自洽的表述框架。

Method: 作者在慢变量流形上内蕴地（坐标无关地）表述平衡测度与Fokker–Planck方程，指出问题源于平衡概率密度所依赖的参考测度（规范）被隐式改变；进而将EH定义为相对于所选规范的标量函数，并对不同EH（常规EH与Riemannian表述中提出的扩散依赖EH）进行规范表示层面的统一比较；同时讨论约束协议如何对应不同参考测度的操作含义，以及宏观极限下大偏差原理的作用。

Result: 证明所谓\"EH定义不良\"并非本质缺陷，而是参考测度（规范）隐式变更所致；常规EH与扩散依赖EH等不同EH实为同一内蕴平衡测度与随机动力学的不同规范表示；规范选择具有可操作意义——不同的慢变量约束协议对应不同的参考测度；当平衡测度在宏观极限下满足大偏差原理时，与次领头规范相关的EH差异相对于大偏差速度成为次领头项。

Conclusion: EH的领头部分由与规范无关的大偏差速率函数确定，相应的确定性动力学亦与规范无关；该工作为有效哈密顿量的定义争议提供了内蕴、几何化的统一解释框架。

Abstract: The effective Hamiltonian (EH), also referred to as the potential of mean force or free-energy landscape, is widely used to describe equilibrium properties and slow dynamics in chemical and softmatter systems. However, the conventional EH has been argued to be ill-defined because it does not transform as a scalar under a change of coordinates. Here, we show that this apparent problem originates from an implicit change of the reference measure (gauge) with respect to which the equilibrium probability density is defined. We formulate the equilibrium measure and the Fokker--Planck equation (FPE) intrinsically on the manifold of slow variables and show that an EH is a scalar function defined with respect to a chosen gauge. Different EHs, including the conventional EH and the diffusion-dependent EH recently proposed in a Riemannian formulation, thus provide different gauge representations of the same intrinsic equilibrium measure and stochastic dynamics. We further show that the gauge choice has an operational meaning: different restraint protocols for the slow variables select different reference measures. Finally, when the equilibrium measure obeys a large-deviation principle in a macroscopic limit, differences among EHs associated with subleading gauges become subleading relative to the large-deviation speed. The leading part of the EH is then determined by the gauge-independent large-deviation rate function, and the corresponding deterministic dynamics is likewise gauge independent.

</details>


### [339] [Interdependent-Network Criticality without a Second Network: Cascading Collapse of the Joule-Coupled Insulator-Metal Transition in $VO_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39155)
*Ouriel Gotesdyner,Michal Wasserman,Hodaya Kaster,Yosi Abulafia,Avital Fried,Bnaya Gross,Shlomo Havlin,Amos Sharoni*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究二维VO₂晶格中电驱动的绝缘体-金属相变，发现其表现为一个相互依存的复杂网络：金属畴的焦耳热耗散显著高于绝缘区，每次开关事件都会抬高网络焦耳热，从而引发级联链式反应。结合时间分辨电阻测量、焦耳耦合电阻网络模型与相互依存网络理论，观察到反常长达数千秒的孕育平台期，并测得临界指数ζ=1/2、β=1/2，与相互依存网络的预测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上对VO₂电驱动绝缘体-金属相变（IMT）的短暂孕育延迟主要采用宏观电热模型分析，缺乏对相变动力学的统计性理解。作者希望揭示这种延迟背后的微观/统计机制。

Method: 在二维VO₂晶格中研究电驱动IMT；结合时间分辨电阻测量、焦耳耦合电阻网络模型（Joule-coupled resistor-network model）以及相互依存网络理论（interdependent-network theory）进行分析。

Result: 观察到一级（突变）相变，其前存在长达数千秒的反常孕育平台期；在该亚稳态平台期，开关活动的有效分支因子在临界点趋近于1，平台寿命以指数ζ=1/2发散，序参量以指数β=1/2标度。

Conclusion: 这些特征与相互依存网络的预测相符，表明仅耗散性的焦耳耦合就足以在单网络关联氧化物体系中驱动混合阶（mixed-order）灾难性级联。

Abstract: When driven by a large enough electric field or current, the insulating state of vanadium dioxide ($VO_2$) typically collapses abruptly into the metallic state (the insulator-metal transition, IMT) following a brief incubation delay. These incubation delays, typically lasting microseconds or less, have been analyzed predominantly through macroscopic electro-thermal modeling, providing little statistical insight into the transition dynamics. Here, we investigate the electrically driven IMT in a two-dimensional $VO_2$ lattice and demonstrate that the material behaves as a complex, interdependent network despite being a single structural entity. Because metallic domains dissipate significantly more Joule heat than insulating regions, each switching event raises the Joule heating of the network, resulting in network dependency and triggering a cascading chain reaction. Combining time-resolved resistance measurements with a Joule-coupled resistor-network model and interdependent-network theory, we observe an abrupt (first-order) transition preceded by an anomalously long incubation plateau lasting thousands of seconds. During this metastable plateau, an effective branching factor of the switching activity approaches unity at criticality, and the plateau lifetime diverges with exponent $ζ=1/2$; the order parameter scales with exponent $β=1/2$. These signatures coincide with those predicted for interdependent networks, indicating that dissipative Joule coupling alone can drive mixed-order catastrophic cascades in a single-network correlated-oxide system.

</details>


### [340] [Excursion-Resolved Thermodynamic Inference without Observing Reverse Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39205)
*Jie Gu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 仅凭有向可见事件的等待时间分布，即可在源-目标闭合条件下重构边界动力学，并给出总熵产生的严格下界；仅观测一个生成有向环即足以辨识完整马尔可夫生成元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验通常只能探测部分跃迁，反向跃迁与隐藏的中间动力学、耗散过程无法直接观测，因此需要一种仅依赖可见事件统计量来推断隐藏动力学与熵产生的方法。

Method: 利用有向可见事件之间的等待时间分布（waiting-time distributions），在简单的源-目标闭合条件（source-target closure condition）下重构边界动力学，确定可见速率以及连接观测态的隐藏短途游走（hidden excursions），并据此导出总熵产生（total entropy production）的严格下界。

Result: 所得的熵产生下界优于在反向事件可获取时使用的标准等待时间估计（standard waiting-time estimate）；且在仅观测到一个生成有向环（spanning directed cycle）的情况下，即使所有反向环边与其他微观跃迁均不可见，也能辨识出完整的马尔可夫生成元（Markov generator）。

Conclusion: 等待时间统计足以从部分观测中恢复隐藏动力学并约束热力学耗散，显著降低了对反向跃迁或全微观跃迁可观测性的要求。

Abstract: Experiments often detect only selected transitions, leaving the underlying state dynamics and dissipation hidden. We show that waiting-time distributions between directed visible events can reconstruct the boundary dynamics without observing the reverse of every detected transition. Under a simple source-target closure condition, they determine visible rates and the hidden excursions connecting observed states, yielding a rigorous lower bound on total entropy production that improves the standard waiting-time estimate when reverse events are available. Remarkably, observing only a spanning directed cycle is sufficient to identify the full Markov generator, even when all reverse cycle edges and other microscopic transitions remain unseen.

</details>


### [341] [Logarithmic Quasi-Long-Range Order and Novel Continuous Phase Transition in the Two-Dimensional $XY$ Model with $1/r^4$ Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39267)
*Xianzhi Pan,Zhijie Fan,Youjin Deng*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究二维经典XY模型在1/r^{2+σ}长程相互作用下的相图与临界性质；在边缘情形σ=2发现低温相为对数准长程序，自旋关联按C(r)~(ln r)^{-η_l}衰减，且η_l∝T；该转变超出GLW范式与BKT机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 明确长程相互作用与短程相互作用两类普适类之间的边界位置，检验近期关于σ=2临界行为的理论预言。

Method: 结合近精确高斯自旋波理论、绝热重整化群分析与大规模蒙特卡洛模拟三种手段。

Result: 1/r^4相互作用以γ(k)≃κk^2 ln(1/k)形式对数修正自旋波刚度核，抑制涡旋增殖，产生非普适关联长度指数ν∝1/√(2πκ)；模拟显示σ≤2时短程代数QLRO相对弱长程扰动不稳定。

Conclusion: 确立σ=2为长程与短程普适类之间的交界，并对近期理论方案给出批判性评估。

Abstract: We investigate the phase diagram and critical properties of the two-dimensional classical XY model with interactions decaying as $1/r^{2+σ}$. At the marginal case $σ=2$, we show that the low-temperature phase is characterized by logarithmic quasi-long-range order (log-QLRO), where spin correlations decay as a power of the logarithm of distance, $C(r)\sim(\ln r)^{-η_\ell}$ with $η_\ell\propto T$, and that the transition into this phase is a continuous transition beyond both the Ginzburg-Landau-Wilson paradigm and the Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless mechanism. Our analysis combines a near-exact Gaussian spin-wave theory, an adiabatic renormalization-group analysis, and large-scale Monte Carlo simulations. We find that the $1/r^4$ interaction logarithmically modifies the spin-wave stiffness kernel as $γ(k)\simeqκk^2\ln(1/k)$, which suppresses vortex proliferation and gives rise to a nonuniversal correlation-length exponent $ν\propto1/\sqrt{2πκ}$. Crucially, our simulations reveal that the short-range algebraic QLRO phase is unstable against a weak long-range perturbation for $σ\le2$. These results establish $σ=2$ as the crossover boundary between the long-range and short-range universality classes and provide a critical evaluation of recent theoretical proposals.

</details>


### [342] [Phase Separation in Fractonic Fluids: Coarsening, Interfaces, Nucleation, and Hyperuniformity in and out of Equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39299)
*Raphael Maire*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作建立了守恒高阶多极矩标量密度流体的相分离理论，系统研究共arsening动力学、成核与界面涨落，发现高阶守恒律普遍使弛豫变慢，而违反涨落-耗散定理则抑制密度与界面涨落并阻碍成核，模拟结果与理论预测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相分离理论多针对守恒标量密度（如模型B）或单一守恒量，而实际流体中密度的高阶多极矩也可能被守恒，其对相分离动力学的影响尚不明确，需要建立相应理论框架。

Method: 构建守恒标量密度高阶多极矩的流体相分离理论（有效场论/动力学方程），分析粗化、成核与界面涨落行为，并结合数值模拟进行验证。

Result: 高阶守恒律使所研究的一切现象弛豫变慢；非平衡情形下违反涨落-耗散定理会抑制密度与界面涨落，同时阻碍成核；模拟结果支持理论预测。

Conclusion: 高阶守恒律与非平衡涨落-耗散违反共同调控相分离的动力学与涨落特性，为理解复杂守恒流体中的相分离提供理论依据。

Abstract: We develop a theory of phase separation in fluids conserving higher-order multipole moments of a scalar density, and investigate the resulting coarsening dynamics, nucleation, and interfacial fluctuations. We find that higher-order conservation laws slow the relaxation of all phenomena investigated, whereas nonequilibrium violations of the fluctuation--dissipation theorem suppress density and interfacial fluctuations while hindering nucleation. Simulations support these predictions.

</details>


### [343] [Square-root growth of operator entanglement in an integrable brickwork circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39401)
*Balázs Pozsgay*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在某四态brickwork线路中构造了算子von Neumann纠缠量为次对数增长的守恒可积模型反例，违反传统预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为可积多体系统无穷体积下局部算子的von Neumann纠缠最多对数增长，本文旨在寻找反例。

Method: 在四态brickwork线路中，以满足常数Yang–Baxter方程的置换矩阵为门，构造可积模型，并分析算子纠缠熵与Rényi熵增长；用矩阵乘积算子模拟评估键维数。

Result: 算子von Neumann熵以(2)√t/π+O(log t)增长；固定指标Rényi熵在指标小于1时线性、大于1时对数增长；固定相对Hilbert–Schmidt误差下，MPO模拟所需键维数至少以exp(c√t)增长。

Conclusion: 该brickwork可积模型给出局部算子纠缠非对数增长的明确反例，且MPO模拟复杂度显著增加，挑战了对可积系统算子纠缠通用增长规律的认识。

Abstract: It is widely expected that the von Neumann entanglement of a local operator grows at most logarithmically in integrable many-body systems in infinite volume. We give a counterexample in a four-state brickwork circuit whose gate is a permutation matrix solving the constant Yang--Baxter equation. The von Neumann operator entropy grows as $(\log2)\sqrt{t/π}+O(\log t)$, whereas fixed-index Rényi entropies grow linearly below index one and logarithmically above it. At fixed relative Hilbert--Schmidt error below one, matrix product operator simulations require a bond dimension growing at least as $\exp(c\sqrt t)$ for some $c>0$.

</details>


### [344] [The Hidden Topology of Network Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39461)
*Debasish Sarker*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文揭示物理网络中拓扑与几何的耦合关系：在剔除拓扑决定的“可交换边对”数目后，边对平均交叉倾向与图结构无关，但波动仍保留拓扑信息；据此提出无参数微观响应理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理网络（如空间嵌入网络）中拓扑与几何通常不可分离，现有归一化指标只看均值，可能掩盖真正决定系统响应的结构信息。作者希望找出一种能剥离拓扑平均效应、同时保留几何-拓扑耦合影响的分析框架。

Method: 先定义“可交换边对”（eligible edge pairs）并按其拓扑决定的数目进行归一化；在此标准化下分析边对交叉倾向（crossing propensity）的均值与波动；进而推导拓扑与几何耦合时响应的无参数微观理论；用合成网络与真实三维网络验证。

Result: 归一化后，边对平均交叉倾向严格与图结构无关（graph independent）；但涨落/相关性仍保持拓扑依赖性；合成与真实三维网络均证实：对归一化均值不可见的结构隐藏在决定响应的相关性中。

Conclusion: 几何-拓扑耦合的响应应由相关结构而非均值描述；提出的无参数理论能捕捉这一效应，提示研究物理网络时需关注涨落与相关性而非仅归一化平均指标。

Abstract: Topology and geometry are inseparable in physical networks. Here we show that, after factoring out the topology-dependent number of eligible edge pairs, exchangeability makes the mean crossing propensity of those pairs exactly graph independent while preserving topology in fluctuations. We derive a microscopic, parameter-free theory for the response when topology and geometry are coupled. Synthetic and real three-dimensional networks confirm that structure hidden from the normalized mean survives in correlations that determine response.

</details>


### [345] [CYNAR: a trajectory-based estimator of entropy production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39654)
*Marco Baiesi,Ivan DI Terlizzi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文提出并验证 CYNAR 流水线，利用熵产生率的动力学分解（traffic 项与 inflow 项）从轨迹估计 σ，并对任意观测子集给出下界，在多系统对比中优于基于稳态概率流的估计，且开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从轨迹推断熵产生率 σ 是随机热力学核心难题：实验中驱动力通常未知，且往往只能观测部分自由度，因此需要不依赖已知力与隐藏变量的估计方法。

Method: 基于 Di Terlizzi (PRL 2025)，发展 CYNAR 流水线：将 σ 做动力学分解为 traffic 项 T（由关联函数的短时曲率推断）与 inflow 项 G（由稳态 score，即对数密度梯度计算）；对任意观测坐标子集评估同一分解，用以给出 σ 下界；并与基于稳态概率流的常规方法在四个复杂度递增系统上对比。

Result: 该分解对任意扩散张量、无需隐藏自由度信息，都能从下方界定 σ。CYNAR 更可靠：近平衡无可见偏置、对空间离散化依赖弱、抗测量噪声强；适用于高维系统（此时仅 traffic 即给出接近 σ 的下界），在部分观测下仍有信息量，包括流基估计恒为零的情形。

Conclusion: CYNAR 是一种稳健、可扩展的熵产生率估计框架，适用于高维与部分观测场景，并已作为开源 Python 包发布。

Abstract: Inferring the entropy production rate $σ$ from recorded trajectories is a key challenge in stochastic thermodynamics. In experiments, the forces governing the dynamics are usually unknown and often only some of the system's degrees of freedom can be observed. Building on a recently introduced method [I. Di Terlizzi, Phys. Rev. Lett. 135, 237101 (2025)], we develop and validate CYNAR (computation yields nonequilibrium analysis and reconstruction), a pipeline that estimates $σ$ from trajectories. It exploits a kinetic decomposition of $σ$ into a traffic term ${\cal T}$, inferred from the short-time curvature of correlation functions, and an inflow rate ${\cal G}$, computed from the steady-state score, i.e., the gradient of the log-density. We show that the same decomposition, evaluated on any subset of observed coordinates, always bounds $σ$ from below, for any diffusion tensor and without knowledge of the hidden degrees of freedom. We then compare CYNAR with a more standard approach that estimates $σ$ from steady-state probability currents, on four systems of increasing complexity. CYNAR proves to be more reliable, with no detectable bias close to equilibrium, weak dependence on spatial discretization, and marked robustness to measurement noise. It is also readily applicable to high-dimensional systems, where the traffic alone provides a lower bound close to $σ$, and remains informative under partial observation, including cases where the flux-based estimate vanishes identically. CYNAR is provided as an open-source Python package.

</details>


### [346] [Minimizing capture time with many small traps in heterogeneous media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39990)
*Denis S. Grebenkov,Theodore Kolokolnikov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究异质介质中大量小吸收陷阱的最优布置以最小化粒子平均首达时间(MFPT)，识别弱捕获与强捕获两个区间，分别给出最优陷阱密度为算术平均与几何平均，并提出统一两者的均匀化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小化扩散粒子被吸收的MFPT在细胞生物学、搜索过程等领域具有应用价值，但异质介质(空间依赖扩散系数D(x)与初始分布ω(x))中大量小陷阱的最优布置缺乏统一理论，经典Green函数方法适用性受限。

Method: 通过理论分析与渐近展开研究二维与三维中陷阱密度优化问题；重新审视经典Green函数方法并界定其仅适用于弱捕获；发展能同时刻画弱、强捕获区间的均匀化方法；并在一维及空间依赖漂移、变截面薄域情形做类似分析，最后以直接数值优化验证。

Result: 二维与三维中识别出两个区间：弱捕获(陷阱较少)时最优陷阱密度为归一化逆扩散率μ∝1/D与初始粒子分布ω的算术平均ρ=½(μ+ω)；强捕获(陷阱较多)时正比于其几何平均ρ∝√(μω)。二维中Green函数方法要求陷阱尺寸随陷阱数指数小，故多数实际情形属强捕获。特别地，当ω∝1/D(Itô意义下无陷阱时的平衡分布)时，最优陷阱分布即稳态粒子分布本身，与捕获强度无关，且使MFPT在整个区域为常数，同时最小化最坏捕获时间。一维中得到ρ∝(μω)^{1/3}。

Conclusion: 该工作统一了弱、强捕获区间的最优陷阱布置理论，明确了经典Green函数方法的适用范围(仅弱捕获)，并揭示ω∝1/D时最优分布的自洽性与鲁棒性；分析可推广至空间依赖漂移与变截面薄域，数值优化证实了解析结论。

Abstract: We study the problem of optimally placing a large number of small absorbing traps to minimize the mean first-passage time (MFPT) of particles diffusing in a heterogeneous medium with space-dependent diffusivity $D(x)$ and prescribed initial particle distribution $ω(x)$. In two and three dimensions we identify two distinct regimes, depending on how strongly the trap ensemble depletes the particles. In the weak trapping regime (fewer traps) the optimal trap density $ρ$ is the arithmetic average $ρ= \frac12(μ+ω)$ of the normalized inverse diffusivity $μ\propto 1/D$ and the initial particle distribution $ω$. In the strong trapping regime (more traps), it is proportional to their geometric average, $ρ\propto \sqrt{μω}$. We revisit the classical Green's function approach and show that it applies to weak trapping only; in 2D this requires the trap size to be exponentially small in the number of traps, so that most applications of interest fall in the strong trapping regime instead. We therefore develop a homogenization approach that captures both regimes. A special choice of initial particle distribution is $ω\propto 1/D$, which corresponds to the equilibrium particle distribution in the absence of traps according to the Itô interpretation. In this case we show that the optimal trap distribution is the stationary particle distribution itself, regardless of the trapping strength, and this makes the MFPT constant throughout the domain; the same trap distribution also minimizes the worst-case capture time. Finally, in one dimension we find instead $ρ\propto\left(μω\right) ^{1/3}$, and a similar analysis covers space-dependent drift and thin domains of variable cross-section. Direct numerical optimization confirms the analytical results.

</details>


### [347] [Many-body topology in parity-preserving tensor networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40004)
*Maksimilian Usoltcev,Nguyen Hanh Dung,Carolin Wille,Matteo Rizzi,Alexander Altland*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究最小两参数平面宇称保持张量网络(ppTN)的收缩，该收缩同时等价于相互作用费米子配分函数、圈气体、形变toric code范数及四次Ising模型。作者用费米子微扰论预测相边界，用张量网络数值确定临界行为，并在圈/自旋对偶下发现一条含c=1四态Potts多临界不动点的自对偶线。核心结构是"拓扑岛"，其边界扭曲Z2指标在Gauss极限外推广了Chern数奇偶性并具圈绕数解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ppTN的费米子表述中，可高效收缩的Gauss极限被局部相互作用破坏，如何用多体方法分析这种非高斯收缩缺乏系统理解；作者希望以最小两参数模型为切入点，借助多方等价表述互补地刻画相图与拓扑结构。

Method: 构造最小两参数ppTN，利用其收缩的多重等价性（相互作用费米子配分函数、圈气体、形变toric-code范数、四次Ising模型）分别求解：用费米子微扰论计算相互作用相边界；用张量网络数值方法确定临界行为；在圈与自旋对偶表述下识别自对偶线；定义边界扭曲Z2指标刻画"拓扑岛"，并证明其退化为Chern数奇偶性及圈绕数解释。

Result: 费米子微扰论给出相互作用的相边界；张量网络数值确定了这些边界的临界行为；圈与自旋描述揭示出一条自对偶线，其上存在c=1的四态Potts多临界不动点。拓扑岛由边界扭曲Z2指标刻画，该指标在Gauss极限内退化为Chern数奇偶性，在有对偶性下具有圈绕数含义。

Conclusion: 该最小ppTN展示了非高斯收缩可通过多种等价表述互补分析，其相图由费米子微扰、张量网络数值及对偶论证一致刻画；边界扭曲Z2指标为拓扑相的刻画提供了超越Gauss极限的不变量，并统一了Chern数与圈绕数视角。

Abstract: Planar parity-preserving tensor networks (ppTNs) admit a fermionic formulation in which the Gaussian, efficiently contractible limit is deformed by local interactions. We investigate how many-body methods can be used to analyze such contractions in a minimal two-parameter ppTN. Its contraction is simultaneously an interacting fermionic partition function, a loop gas, a deformed toric-code norm, and a quartic Ising model, allowing the same phase diagram to be approached with complementary tools. At its center lies a `topological island', characterized beyond the Gaussian limit by a boundary-twist $\mathbb Z_2$ indicator that reduces to Chern-number parity and admits a loop-winding interpretation under duality. Fermionic perturbation theory predicts the interacting phase boundaries, tensor-network numerics establish their critical behavior, and the loop and spin descriptions reveal a self-dual line with a $c=1$ four-state-Potts multicritical fixed point.

</details>


### [348] [Spectral theory for dynamical large deviations in non-Markov self-interacting processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40329)
*Francesco Coghi,Juan P. Garrahan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文为非马尔可夫跳跃过程与非马尔可夫链建立动力学大偏差的谱理论，利用多尺度WKBJ拟设将Feynman-Kac方程约化为倾斜算子的本征值问题，并结合Hamilton-Jacobi特征线实现高效涨落量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非马尔可夫动力学依赖通过状态和跳跃相关经验可观测量体现的历史记忆，传统马尔可夫大偏差谱理论难以直接适用，因此需要一套能同时处理快构型弛豫与慢记忆演化的谱框架。

Method: 针对非马尔可夫跳跃过程和链，采用多尺度Wentzel-Kramers-Brillouin-Jeffreys (WKBJ) 拟设，将描述占据与流统计的Feynman-Kac方程约化为一个与Hamilton-Jacobi特征线耦合的新倾斜算子的本征值问题。

Result: 该约化将快构型弛豫与慢记忆演化解耦，给出了可计算的非马尔可夫涨落量化框架；并以双稳自诱导East模型作为示例验证了一般结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的谱理论为广泛非马尔可夫系统中的大偏差涨落提供了计算高效的通用工具，并可通过具体模型（如双稳自诱导East模型）加以应用。

Abstract: We develop a spectral theory for dynamical large deviations in non-Markov jump processes and non-Markov chains, whose dynamics depends on the past through state- and jump-dependent empirical observables. We demonstrate that a multiscale Wentzel-Kramers-Brillouin-Jeffreys (WKBJ) Ansatz separates fast configurational relaxation from slow memory evolution, reducing the Feynman--Kac equation for occupation and flux statistics to an eigenvalue problem for a new tilted operator coupled to Hamilton--Jacobi characteristics. This provides a computationally efficient framework for quantifying fluctuations in a broad class of non-Markovian systems. We illustrate our general results with a bistable self-induced East model.

</details>


<div id='nlin.AO'></div>

# nlin.AO [[Back]](#toc)

### [349] [Recovery Random Walks and Extreme Events on Complex Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39377)
*Karan Singh,Narendran R. V.,V. K. Chandrasekar,D. V. Senthilkumar*

Main category: nlin.AO

TL;DR: 提出恢复随机游走（RRW）模型：极端事件使发生节点"冻结"固定时长Δ，捕获游走者并减少有效移动种群，冻结比例出现初始过冲后阻尼振荡趋于稳态，冻结抑制极端事件概率与频率但保留其度依赖动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统极端事件研究基于简单随机游走框架，其概率仅由网络结构与游走者稳态分布决定，忽视了极端事件对节点本身的时间性影响，缺乏现实性。

Method: 在随机游走上引入"冻结"机制：极端事件发生处节点被冻结时长Δ（Δ=0退化为原模型），游走者被捕获、有效移动种群下降。从第一性原理刻画该反馈，并推导冻结节点比例的时滞微分方程。

Result: 有限冻结时间导致冻结节点比例先出现初始过冲，随后阻尼振荡松弛至稳态；冻结降低极端事件概率与频率，同时保持其度依赖动力学；冻结节点比例的理论预测与仿真结果吻合良好。

Conclusion: RRW模型更贴近现实场景，能给出更具实用价值的极端事件统计特性。

Abstract: Extreme events are widely studied within simple random walk frameworks, where their probability is determined by the network structure and stationary walker distribution. Here, we propose a recovery random walk (RRW) model in which extreme events temporally `freeze' the nodes where they occur for a fixed duration $Δ$ ($Δ=0$ recovers the original model), trapping walkers and reducing the effective mobile population, thereby making the model more practical. We derive a first-principles description of this feedback and a delayed differential equation for the frozen-node fraction. This finite freezing produces an initial overshoot, followed by damped oscillatory relaxation to a steady state for the fraction of the frozen nodes. We find that freezing suppresses extreme event probability while preserving its degree dependence dynamics, and suppresses the EE frequency. Further, the analytical prediction for the fraction of the frozen nodes agrees closely with simulations. This model closely reflects real-world scenarios, yielding more practical EE statistics.

</details>


### [350] [Dimension-dependent order parameter selection in higher-order Kuramoto dynamics on spheres: continuous versus quantized regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39728)
*Hyungjin Huh,Dohyun Kim*

Main category: nlin.AO

TL;DR: 本文研究含吸引成对耦合与排斥高阶效应的高维Kuramoto模型，刻画了球面上平衡态（balanced equilibria）的吸引域、非平衡态的线性不稳定性，以及圆上因种群不平衡产生的两簇锁相态及其连续-量子化序参量选择机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成对耦合倾向于同步，而高阶交互阻止完全同步并选择中间相干水平。作者希望理解这种竞争如何在高维球面与一维圆上产生不同的稳定态选择机制。

Method: 针对维度≥2的单位球面，显式构造平衡态吸引域并分类非零均值平衡态、分析线性稳定性；在圆上将该模型约化为高阶Kuramoto型模型，构造两簇锁相态的吸引域，识别有限N下序参量的选择值，并通过根选择机制解释两簇锁相态的普遍出现及大幅种群不平衡锁相态的线性不稳定性。

Result: 球面上平衡态具有显式吸引域，所有非平衡平衡态线性不稳定，平衡态是高维中唯一自然稳定候选；圆上有限N的种群不平衡阻止精确静止平衡，产生共同角漂移与锁相态，两簇锁相态被构造出吸引域并给出有限N序参量选择值，大幅种群不平衡的两簇锁相态线性不稳定。

Conclusion: 该模型展现出“连续-量子化序参量选择”现象：高维与圆上的稳定态选择机制本质不同且依赖维度，只有部分锁相态具有动态鲁棒性。

Abstract: We study a high-dimensional Kuramoto model with attractive pairwise coupling and a repulsive higher-order effect. Although the pairwise interaction favors synchronization, the higher-order interaction prevents complete synchronization and selects an intermediate level of coherence corresponding to the balanced equilibria. For the unit sphere of dimension at least two, we provide an explicit basin of attraction for the balanced equilibria. We also classify nonzero-mean equilibria and show that all non-balanced equilibria are linearly unstable. This indicates that balanced states are the only natural stable candidates in the higher dimensions.
  In contrast, on the circle, the same model reduces to a higher-order Kuramoto-type model which exhibits a qualitatively different selection mechanism that depends fundamentally on the dimension. In this case, population imbalance at finite $N$ prevents exact stationary balance and instead generates a common angular drift producing phase-locked states. We construct basins of attraction for two-cluster locked states, in which the phases split into two groups, and identify the corresponding finite-$N$ selected values of the order parameters. This phenomenon is called continuous-versus-quantized order-parameter selection. We further demonstrate, through a root-selection mechanism, why such two-cluster locked states are typically observed in most simulations. Finally, we show that two-cluster locked states with a large population imbalance are linearly unstable, which explains why only certain locked states are dynamically robust.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [351] [Complex structures on $S^6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38442)
*Philip Engel*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文围绕近期由Claude在Levent Alpöge指导下构造的一类与S^6微分类的紧复三维流形族展开，旨在阐释其几何思想、给出自包含证明并讨论未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期出现了一类与S^6微分类的紧复三维流形单参数族构造，该构造来源特殊且结果重要，但背后的几何直观与证明细节需要系统梳理，因此本文希望揭示其几何思想并提供完整证明，同时指出后续可探索的问题。

Method: 文章采用几何构造与证明相结合的方式：描述该单参数族紧复三维流形的构造思路，梳理其中的几何思想，并给出自包含的证明。

Result: 给出了与S^6微分类的紧复三维流形单参数族的几何解释和自包含证明，并附有对未来研究方向的评述。

Conclusion: 该工作阐明并验证了相关紧复三维流形族的构造，为后续研究提供了几何框架和可能的研究方向。

Abstract: A recent manuscript produced by Claude under the direction of Levent Alpöge constructs a 1-parameter family of compact complex threefolds diffeomorphic to $S^6$. Our goal is to describe the geometric ideas behind the construction, give a self-contained proof, and remark on possible future research directions.

</details>


### [352] [Zero velocity Lagrangians in the Hitchin system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38548)
*Peter Gothen,André Oliveira,Rodrigo Pereira*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 构造一类新的 Higgs 丛模空间拉格朗日子簇，计算其与 Hitchin 纤维的交点，并构造对偶模空间上的向量丛以猜想其镜像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Higgs 丛模空间的拉格朗日子簇是镜像对称研究的基础对象，需构造新类型拉格朗日并探索其镜像对应。

Method: 在模空间上利用非零复数的自然作用施加"零速度条件"（受无穷小余法原理启发），构造纤维化于 C*-不动点子簇之上的拉格朗日子簇。

Result: 证明了该拉格朗日与 Hitchin 映射一般纤维交点数的公式，并在对偶模空间上构造了一个向量丛。

Conclusion: 该拉格朗日类似 [CW19] 和 [HH22] 的上行流；所构造向量丛被猜想对应其镜像超全纯丛。

Abstract: Lagrangians of the moduli space of Higgs bundles are a fundamental piece of its study under the lens of mirror symmetry. We construct a new class of Lagrangians defined via a zero 'velocity condition' imposed on the natural action of the non-zero complex numbers on the moduli space, motivated by a certain 'infinitesimal conormal principle'. These are Lagrangian subvarieties analogous to the upward flows considered by [CW19] and [HH22], but which fiber over the $\mathbb{C}^*$-fixed point subvarieties. We then prove a formula for the number of intersection points of this Lagrangian with the generic fiber of the Hitchin map, and build a vector bundle over the dual moduli space which we conjecture to correspond to its mirror hyperholomorphic bundle.

</details>


### [353] [Intrinsic mirror symmetry for orbifold cubic surfaces and the $A_1$ spherical DAHA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38556)
*Pierrick Bousseau,Sayan Chattopadhyay*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文确定含三个节点的orbifold三次曲面的intrinsic mirror，并将其几何通过一次穿孔环面的PGL(2,C)与SL(2,C) character varieties之间的镜像对称加以解释；进一步用高亏格Gromov-Witten理论构造该mirror的量子化，证明其可恢复A_1球面double affine Hecke代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 围绕orbifold镜像对称与character varieties的联系，作者希望在含节点的orbifold三次曲面这一具体几何上确定其intrinsic mirror，并建立与一次穿孔环面character varieties镜像对应的几何解释，同时探索其量子化结构与已知代数结构的关系。

Method: 通过确定含三个节点的orbifold三次曲面的intrinsic mirror；利用一次穿孔环面的PGL(2,C)与SL(2,C) character varieties之间的镜像对称解释所得几何；再借助高亏格Gromov-Witten理论构造该mirror的量子化。

Result: 得到了该orbifold三次曲面的intrinsic mirror及其基于character varieties镜像对称的几何解释；构造出mirror的量子化，并证明该量子化恢复出A_1球面double affine Hecke代数。

Conclusion: 该工作将orbifold镜像对称、character varieties与高亏格Gromov-Witten量子化联系起来，并揭示量子化结构对应A_1球面double affine Hecke代数。

Abstract: We determine the intrinsic mirror of an orbifold cubic surface with three nodes and interpret the resulting geometry through mirror symmetry between the $PGL(2,\mathbb{C})$ and $SL(2,\mathbb{C})$ character varieties of the once-punctured torus. We further construct a quantization of the mirror using higher genus Gromov-Witten theory and show that it recovers the $A_1$ spherical double affine Hecke algebra.

</details>


### [354] [Finite automorphism groups of hypersurfaces via Bott vanishing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38572)
*Dominic Bunnett,Caroline Namanya*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文给出了光滑Deligne-Mumford栈中超曲面的自同构群概型在tame特征下离散的上同调判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超曲面自同构群概型何时是离散的（即无穷小自同构消失）是代数几何中的重要问题，尤其tame特征下的加权射影超曲面情形缺乏统一处理。

Method: 通过将环境栈上的Bott型消没定理转化为线性系统中每个quasismooth超曲面无穷小自同构的消没，建立上同调判据；并将其应用于toric orbifold。

Result: 得到了tame特征下quasismooth加权射影超曲面以及射影空间乘积中超曲面的统一有限性结果，并精确刻画了该方法失效的例外窗口。

Conclusion: 该上同调判据为超曲面自同构离散性提供了有效工具，并明确了方法的适用范围与边界。

Abstract: We give a cohomological criterion for the automorphism group scheme of a hypersurface in a smooth Deligne-Mumford stack to be discrete in tame characteristics. The criterion converts Bott-type vanishing on the ambient stack into vanishing of infinitesimal automorphisms of every quasismooth hypersurface in a linear system. We apply it to toric orbifolds, obtain uniform finiteness results for quasismooth weighted-projective hypersurfaces in tame characteristic and hypersurfaces in products of projective spaces, and identify the precise exceptional windows in which the method fails.

</details>


### [355] [The Bias Conjecture for One-Parameter, Non-Isotrivial Elliptic Curve Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38664)
*Lucas Chen,Joshua Im,Steven J. Miller,Devayani Pradhan*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文研究一般单参数椭圆曲线族（j-不变量非常数）上的 Miller 偏差猜想，通过将二阶矩分解为不同上同调分量，分别处理 p^{3/2}、p 及更低阶项，最终证明 p 项系数在极限下平均为严格负值，从而确立该猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Miller 偏差猜想关注椭圆曲线族的某些统计量在极限下是否呈现系统性偏向，但此前缺乏对一般单参数族、非常数 j-不变量的普遍证明。本文旨在为该类族建立该猜想。

Method: 采用 Michel 的方法，将二阶矩拆解为：主 p^2 项、来自射影直线上第一上同调的 p^{3/2} 项、以及对应奇异纤维的 p 阶或更低阶项。在广义 Sato-Tate 猜想假设下，运用表示论与 motive 理论证明 p^{3/2} 项系数平均为零；随后不依赖 Sato-Tate，独立分析低阶贡献，借助 Chebotarev 密度定理与 Galois 表示论证可加奇异纤维对 p 阶项的贡献平均也为零。

Result: p^{3/2} 项系数在极限下平均为零；可加奇异纤维对 p 阶项的贡献平均为零；p 项剩余平均贡献为 -B_p p，其中 B_p 为判别式消失的“坏”点数目。

Conclusion: 由于 B_p 的平均值为严格正，p 项总体系数在极限下平均为严格负，由此对这类单参数椭圆曲线族证明了 Miller 偏差猜想。

Abstract: We investigate Miller's bias conjecture for general one-parameter families of elliptic curves over $\mathbb{Q}$ with non-constant $j$-invariants. Utilizing methods developed by Michel, we view the second moment as the sum of distinct cohomological components: a main $p^2$ term, a $p^{3/2}$ term arising from the first cohomology over the projective line, and terms of order $p$ or lower corresponding to singular fibers. By assuming the generalized Sato-Tate conjecture, we employ representation and motive theory to demonstrate that the coefficient of the $p^{3/2}$ term averages to zero in the limit. We then independently analyze the lower-order contributions without assuming Sato-Tate. By applying the Chebotarev density theorem and analyzing Galois representations, we show that the contributions to the order $p$ term from additive singular fibers also average to zero. Consequently, the only remaining average contribution to the $p$ term is $-B_pp$, where $B_p$ denotes the number of ``bad'' points where the discriminant vanishes. Because $B_p$ averages to a strictly positive value, the overall coefficient of the $p$ term averages to a strictly negative value in the limit, thereby establishing the bias conjecture for these families.

</details>


### [356] [Supersingular Tate conjecture for irreducible symplectic varieties of known types: I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38801)
*Lie Fu,Xuanlin Huang,Zhiyuan Li*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在可提升到特征零的条件下，若干类型的超奇异不可约辛簇具有 Tate Chow  motive（K3^[n]、OG6、OG10）或超奇异 Abel Chow motive（Kum^n），并由此对其乘积推出全上同调环的超奇异 Tate 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超奇异 Tate 猜想是代数几何中的重要问题，此前对一般不可约辛簇（及其乘积）尚不清楚。该文旨在通过有限制的提升条件，验证特定变形类型不可约辛簇的动机结构，从而推进该猜想。

Method: 利用超奇异不可约辛簇可提升到特征零的假设，结合 Tate Chow motive 与 Abel Chow motive 的理论，按变形类型（K3^[n]、OG6、OG10、Kum^n）分别分析，并对 Artin 不变量施加排除条件。

Result: K3^[n]、OG6（Artin 不变量≠4）、OG10（Artin 不变量≠12）型具有 Tate Chow motive；Kum^n 型（Artin 不变量≠3）具有超奇异 Abel Chow motive。作为推论，这些簇的乘积满足 ℓ-adic 与晶体上同调整环的超奇异 Tate 猜想。

Conclusion: 在提升假设与特定 Artin 不变量条件下，多类超奇异不可约辛簇及其乘积满足超奇异 Tate 猜想，推广了该猜想在不可约辛簇上的适用范围。

Abstract: We prove that for a supersingular irreducible symplectic variety admitting suitable lifting to characteristic zero has Tate Chow motive if it is of deformation type $K3^{[n]}$, OG6 (with Artin invariant $\neq$ 4), or OG10 (with Artin invariant $\neq$ 12), and has supersingular abelian Chow motive if it is of $\mathrm{Kum}^n$-type (with Artin invariant $\neq$ 3). In particular, any product of those irreducible symplectic varieties satisfies the supersingular Tate conjecture for the whole $\ell$-adic or crystalline cohomology ring.

</details>


### [357] [Divisors and harmonic morphisms on metric Graphs of pseudocompact type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38821)
*Benjamin Mascuch,Montserrat Teixidor i Bigas*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文给出伪紧致型度量图上除子$d$-亏格的等价刻画：识别平行边得到树时，除子$d$-亏格等价于存在到树的度$d$调和态射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作受代数曲线中紧致型曲线的极限线性系统与容许覆盖之间对应的启发，希望在度量图这一组合框架下建立类似的对偶/对应关系，并推进正秩除子的提升问题及Brill-Noether理论。

Method: 针对伪紧致型度量图（识别平行边后为树），采用构造性证明方法建立除子$d$-亏格与到树的度$d$调和态射之间的等价；进一步推导正秩除子的提升结果，并在识别平行边得到路径的情形下给出保持亏格的细化；最后研究路径情形下的Brill-Noether理论。

Result: 证明了伪紧致型图上除子$d$-亏格等价于存在到树的度$d$调和态射，并得到正秩除子的提升结果，在路径情形下获得保持亏格的细化，同时给出了路径情形下的Brill-Noether理论。

Conclusion: 该结果为度量图的除子理论与调和态射之间建立了构造性联系，呼应了紧致型曲线中极限线性系统与容许覆盖的代数对应，并为正秩除子提升和路径情形的Brill-Noether理论提供了新工具。

Abstract: A metric graph is of pseudocompact type if identifying parallel edges produces a tree. We give a constructive proof that, for such graphs, divisorial $d$-gonality is equivalent to the existence of a degree $d$ harmonic morphism to a tree. This mirrors the algebraic correspondence, for curves of compact type, between limit linear series of dimension one and admissible covers. We also deduce lifting results for positive-rank divisors, with a genus-preserving refinement when identifying parallel edges produces a path. Finally, we study the Brill-Noether theory in the path case.

</details>


### [358] [On algebraic dynamics of birational automorphisms of Calabi-Yau manifolds and the universal Coxeter groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38906)
*Keiji Oguiso*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文延续复Calabi-Yau流形双有理自同构代数动力学的研究，聚焦本原性与Zariski稠密轨道的存在性，并强调万有Coxeter群及其Coxeter元素的重要作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续先前关于复Calabi-Yau流形双有理自同构代数动力学的研究，围绕本原性（primitivity）与Zariski稠密轨道的存在性这两个核心问题展开，并借助万有Coxeter群及Coxeter元素这一代数工具推进研究。

Method: 以复Calabi-Yau流形的双有理自同构为对象，从代数动力学视角研究其本原性与Zariski稠密轨道的存在性；引入万有Coxeter群及其Coxeter元素作为关键分析工具。

Result: 摘要未给出具体结果，仅说明该工作为前期研究的延续，并指出万有Coxeter群及其Coxeter元素在其中起重要作用。

Conclusion: 复Calabi-Yau流形双有理自同构的代数动力学研究可借助万有Coxeter群与Coxeter元素来刻画本原性及Zariski稠密轨道的存在性，该方向具有延续性与拓展价值。

Abstract: This is a continuation of our study of algebraic dymanics of birational automorphisms of complex Calabi-Yau manifolds in the view of primitivity and the existence of Zariski dense orbit. The universal Coxeter groups and their Coxeter elements also play important roles in our study.

</details>


### [359] [Structure of higher-genus open-closed Gromov--Witten theory of $\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^{1}}(p-1)\oplus\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^{1}}(-p-1)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40257)
*Shuai Guo,Jingyi Xu,Qingsheng Zhang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了环面Calabi–Yau三维流形 $X_p=\operatorname{Tot}(\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^1}(p-1)\oplus\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^1}(-p-1))$（$p\geq 2$）的闭与开Gromov–Witten势，在环面权非零条件下证明了闭与开镜像对称，并将其与镜像曲线上的拓扑递归相联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于该族局部环面Calabi–Yau三维流形，其闭、开Gromov–Witten势的镜像对称与拓扑递归结构（尤其高亏格与双标度极限）尚缺乏统一处理；作者还希望证明Caporaso–Griguolo–Mariño–Pasquetti–Seminara提出的高亏格拟设与双标度猜想。

Method: 在环面权非零条件下，利用镜像对称将闭、开Gromov–Witten势对应于镜像曲线上的拓扑递归；进而分析递归解的代数结构，并构造相应的双标度极限。

Result: 证明了闭与开镜像对称；建立了高亏格闭势与稳定开势的多项式结构；给出了镜像曲线上拓扑递归的双标度极限。作为推论，闭势的结果证明了Caporaso–Griguolo–Mariño–Pasquetti–Seminara的高亏格拟设与双标度猜想。

Conclusion: 该工作统一刻画了 $X_p$ 的闭、开Gromov–Witten势的镜像对称与拓扑递归性质，验证了已有高亏格拟设和双标度猜想，并为相关局部Calabi–Yau的枚举几何提供了多项式结构与极限行为的框架。

Abstract: We study the closed and open Gromov--Witten potentials of the toric Calabi--Yau threefold $$ X_p=\operatorname{Tot}\bigl(\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^{1}}(p-1)\oplus\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^{1}}(-p-1)\bigr),\qquad p\geq 2. $$ We prove closed and open mirror symmetry under a nonvanishing condition on the torus weights, relating these potentials to topological recursion on the mirror curves. We establish polynomial structures for both the higher-genus closed potentials and the stable open potentials. We also establish double-scaling limits for topological recursion on the mirror curves. In particular, our results for the closed potentials prove the higher-genus ansatz and the double-scaling conjecture of Caporaso--Griguolo--Mariño--Pasquetti--Seminara.

</details>


### [360] [Quasi-Projectivity of Weighted Right-Angled Artin Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39104)
*Yongqiang Liu,Xiaobin Xu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文分类了可作为光滑复拟射影簇基本群的加权直角Artin群，证明它们恰为有限生成自由群与群 ⟨a,b∣[a,b]^m=1⟩ 的有限直积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将直角Artin群推广为带权版本，并刻画其中哪些能实现为光滑复拟射影簇的基本群，以理解这类几何基本群的代数特征。

Method: 在定义图的边上赋予权重来构造加权直角Artin群，并对可作为光滑复拟射影簇基本群的该类群进行代数分类。

Result: 可作为光滑复拟射影簇基本群的加权直角Artin群恰为有限生成自由群与群 ⟨a,b∣[a,b]^m=1⟩ 的有限直积。

Conclusion: 给出了此类可几何实现的加权直角Artin群的完整分类，表明其结构被限制为有限直积形式。

Abstract: Weighted right-angled Artin groups generalize right-angled Artin groups by adding weights to the edges of the defining graphs. We classify the weighted right-angled Artin groups that occur as fundamental groups of smooth complex quasi-projective varieties. They are precisely the finite direct products of finitely generated free groups and groups of the form $\langle a,b\mid [a,b]^m=1\rangle$.

</details>


### [361] [Canonical representatives and interval independence in Ekedahl's equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39293)
*Yuan Yang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在总化之前研究Ekedahl等价（完成导出函子）的逆，构造有限投射消解，将逆表示为与双模复形的张量积，给出对角上同调的实际R-复形代表，证明其与区间无关，并得到从对角心到Ch(R)的精确全忠实函子，分裂局部化；最后计算了三类超一般阿贝尔三维簇的循环晶体表示的domino因子，初等因子型为{2,3,4}、{2,3,7}、{2,3,6}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ekedahl等价通过完成的导出函子从凝聚F-gauges重构凝聚Raynaud复形，但其逆函子在总化之前的结构与性质尚不清晰。作者希望在这一层面显式构造逆函子、获得对角上同调的复形代表，并理解与之相关的局部化分裂现象。

Method: 在固定有限层级上构造有限投射消解，并将Ekedahl逆表示为与一个双模复形的张量积；沿消解方向取上同调得到对角上同调的实际R-复形代表，并证明其与区间选取无关；进而建立从对角心到Ch(R)的精确全忠实函子以分裂局部化；最后对超一般阿贝尔三维簇的三类循环晶体表示计算domino因子。

Result: 证明了R-复形代表不依赖于区间，给出了分裂局部化的精确全忠实函子（对角心→Ch(R)）；对三类循环晶体表示计算得domino（初等）因子型分别为{2,3,4}、{2,3,7}与{2,3,6}。

Conclusion: Ekedahl逆在总化前可由有限投射消解与双模复形张量积显式刻画，其消解方向上同调给出与区间无关的对角上同调R-复形代表，并产生分裂局部化的全忠实函子；超一般阿贝尔三维簇的循环晶体表示具有上述具体domino因子型。

Abstract: Ekedahl's equivalence reconstructs coherent Raynaud complexes from coherent F-gauges by a completed derived functor. We study this inverse before totalization. At a fixed finite level, we construct finite projective resolutions and express the inverse as a tensor product with a complex of bimodules. Its cohomology in the resolution direction gives actual R-complex representatives of diagonal cohomology. We prove that these representatives are independent of the interval and obtain an exact fully faithful functor from the diagonal heart to Ch(R), splitting localization. Finally, we compute the domino factors for three cyclic crystalline presentations of supergeneral abelian threefolds. The resulting elementary factor types are {2,3,4}, {2,3,7}, and {2,3,6}.

</details>


### [362] [Normal Bundle Splitting Strata of Rational Curves in Toric Varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39309)
*Hikari Iwasaki*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文研究光滑射影环面簇中固定类的无分歧有理曲线族的法丛分裂型分层，利用Cox构造给出相对法丛的两项消解与相对上同调态射，得到非平衡性的数值判别准则，并应用于沿线性子空间爆破射影空间的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解有理曲线族中法丛分裂型的分层结构，特别是判断法丛是否非平衡（unbalanced），对研究模空间与Gromov-Witten理论中的退化现象具有重要意义。

Method: 使用Cox构造，在固定源参数空间上的万有曲线处，用线丛直和构造相对法丛的显式两项消解；进而构造参数空间上向量丛的态射，称为相对上同调态射，用于研究一般分裂型与跳跃轨迹。

Result: 得到法丛非平衡性的充分数值判别准则；将其应用于沿线性子空间爆破射影空间的情形，证明该数值准则与Cela--Lian的结果等价；并给出跳跃轨迹期望Chow类的显式公式。

Conclusion: 该工作为环面簇中有理曲线族的法丛分裂型提供了构造性且可计算的框架，统一并推广了已有非平衡性判别方法，并给出跳跃轨迹Chow类的显式描述。

Abstract: We study stratification by normal-bundle splitting type in families of unramified rational curves of fixed class in a smooth projective toric variety over an algebraically closed field {of arbitrary characteristic}. Using the Cox construction, we construct an explicit two-term resolution of the relative normal bundle by direct sums of line bundles on the universal curve over the fixed-source parameter space. Then we construct morphisms of vector bundles on the parameter space, which we call relative cohomology morphisms, from which we can investigate generic splitting type and jumping loci of the family. In particular, we obtain sufficient numerical criteria for unbalancedness of the normal bundle. We apply this method to blowups of projective space along linear subspaces, and show that the numerical criteria for unbalancedness are equivalent to those of Cela--Lian \cite{CelaLian2026}. We also obtain explicit formulas for the expected Chow classes of the jump loci.

</details>


### [363] [Quivers variety for affine orthogonal quiver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39434)
*Victor Pinot*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究正交箭图的箭图簇，通过预投射代数中的图解计算，确定其底层图为仿射Dynkin图时能得到哪些Kleinian曲面奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿射Dynkin箭图在Kronheimer构造及Nakajima箭图簇表述中具有特殊地位，本文希望将这一经典框架推广到正交箭图情形，刻画其与Kleinian曲面奇点的对应关系。

Method: 针对正交箭图定义相应的箭图簇，并在该箭图的预投射代数中建立一套图解计算（graphical calculus）方法，用以判定哪些Kleinian曲面奇点可由底层图为仿射Dynkin图的正交箭图产生。

Result: 通过图解计算，确定了哪些Kleinian曲面奇点可由底层图分别为仿射Dynkin图的正交箭图得到，给出了正交箭图情形下的完整分类。

Conclusion: 正交箭图可产生特定类型的Kleinian曲面奇点，其结果构成经典Kronheimer–Nakajima对应在正交情形下的推广。

Abstract: We study quiver varieties associated with orthogonal quivers and determine the Kleinian surface singularities arising from affine Dynkin types. In the classical setting, affine Dynkin quivers play a distinguished role through Kronheimer's construction of Kleinian singularities and its reformulation by Nakajima in terms of quiver varieties. Using a graphical calculus in the preprojective algebra of the quiver, we determine which Kleinian surface singularities arise from orthogonal quivers whose underlying graph is an affine Dynkin diagram.

</details>


### [364] [Non-commutative Fano varieties and three-dimensional minifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39766)
*Konstantin Loginov,Dmitri Orlov*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文引入非交换 Fano 簇与非交换极小簇概念，在三维情形下研究后者，证明生成几何螺旋的完全例外集合的 Euler 矩阵恰构成五个变异类，Euler 度分别为 22、40、54、64、72，其中前四类由交换 Fano 三维簇实现，第五类由非交换族 \(M_{72}\) 实现且不能由光滑射影三维簇实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Fano 簇与极小簇理论在双有理几何中具有核心地位，而完全例外集合的 Euler 矩阵刻画了导出范畴的几何性质。借助非交换 Fano 簇与非交换极小簇的概念，将这一框架推广到非交换情形，以探索交换三维簇所无法实现的例外集合类型，从而揭示非交换几何中新的变异类。

Method: 首先给出非交换 Fano 簇与非交换 minifold 的定义；随后在三维情形下，以生成几何螺旋的完全例外集合为对象，计算其 Euler 矩阵，并按变异等价关系对所得矩阵进行分类。

Result: 三维非交换极小簇上生成几何螺旋的完全例外集合的 Euler 矩阵恰好分为五个变异类，其 Euler 度分别为 22、40、54、64、72；其中前四类分别由交换 Fano 三维簇 \(X_{22},V_5,Q^3,\mathbb P^3\) 实现；第五类由非交换族 \(M_{72}\) 实现，且无法由光滑射影三维簇上的完全例外集合实现。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了三维非交换极小簇的 Euler 矩阵分类，表明非交换框架下存在交换光滑射影三维簇无法实现的例外集合变异类，凸显了 \(M_{72}\) 作为本质非交换例子的意义，并为高维非交换双有理几何与导出范畴研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: We introduce notions of non-commutative Fano varieties and non-commutative minifolds, and study the latter in dimension three. We prove that the Euler matrices of full exceptional collections generating geometric helices on these minifolds form five mutation classes, distinguished by their Euler degrees \(22,40,54,64,72.\) The first four classes are realized by (commutative) Fano threefolds \(X_{22},V_5,Q^3,\mathbb P^3,\) respectively. The fifth is realized by the non-commutative family \(M_{72}\) and cannot be realized by a full exceptional collection on a smooth projective threefold.

</details>


### [365] [Potential isotriviality of isocrystals and proper covers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39815)
*Adrian Langer,Lei Zhang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究在沿真满射态射拉回后变为平凡（trivial）的收敛与超收敛等晶体（isocrystal）；在几何单枝（geometrically unibranch）簇上，此类对象总能被有限étale覆盖平凡化；在完美域上可用有限étale覆盖替代几何平凡化的真覆盖，并推出几何单值群有限；对真几何单枝簇，几何单值群有限也是充分条件；超收敛情形下可用支配态射替代真覆盖；用结点曲线说明几何单枝假设的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题：当等晶体在某个真满射态射拉回后变为平凡时，能否用更"好"的覆盖（有限étale覆盖）实现平凡化？这关联到几何单值群的有限性，以及在收敛/超收敛框架下覆盖结构的可替换性问题。

Method: 设V为代数闭域上几何单枝簇，X为收敛或超收敛等晶体。假设存在真满射f: Y→V使f*X平凡，通过分析此类平凡化条件并结合几何单枝性质，构造有限étale覆盖实现X的平凡化。对超收敛情形，进一步放宽为支配态射。以结点曲线作为反例检验几何单枝假设的不可或缺性。

Result: 1) 几何单枝簇上，任何被真覆盖平凡化的等晶体已被有限étale覆盖平凡化；2) 完美域上，几何平凡化的真覆盖可替换为基域上的有限étale覆盖，且几何单值群有限；3) 对真几何单枝簇，几何单值群有限亦为充分条件；4) 超收敛情形下，可用支配态射替代给定真覆盖；5) 结点曲线表明几何单枝假设不可去除。

Conclusion: 等晶体的几何平凡化可由有限étale覆盖实现，从而给出几何单值群有限性的充分必要条件（对真几何单枝簇）；几何单枝条件是关键，结点曲线显示了其必要性；超收敛情形具有更强的覆盖替换性质。

Abstract: We study convergent and overconvergent isocrystals that become trivial after pullback along a proper surjective morphism. On a geometrically unibranch variety over an algebraically closed field, every such object is already trivialized by a finite étale cover. Over a perfect field, this shows that a proper cover giving geometric triviality can be replaced by a finite étale cover over the ground field, and implies finiteness of the geometric monodromy group. For proper geometrically unibranch varieties, finite geometric monodromy is also sufficient. In the overconvergent case, a dominant morphism can replace the given proper cover. A nodal curve shows why the geometrically unibranch hypothesis is needed.

</details>


### [366] [On the quadratic length of plane Cremona maps of degree 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39943)
*Alberto Calabri,Nguyen Thi Ngoc Giao*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究四次平面 Cremona 映射的二次长度：非 de Jonquières 型二次长度为 2；de Jonquières 型二次长度为 3、4 或 5，并对达到上界 5 的映射进行了分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面 Cremona 映射可分解为二次映射，其最少分解个数称为二次长度。三次映射的二次长度已知为 2 或 3，而四次情形尚待刻画，本文旨在确定四次平面 Cremona 映射的二次长度并分类极端情形。

Method: 按 de Jonquières 与非 de Jonquières 两类对四次平面 Cremona 映射 $\varphi$ 进行分类讨论，分别确定其二次长度；对 de Jonquières 型进一步分析并分类二次长度达到上界者。

Result: 非 de Jonquières 的四次映射二次长度为 2；de Jonquières 映射（即含三重基点的映射）二次长度为 3、4 或 5，并给出了二次长度为 5 的映射的完整分类。

Conclusion: 四次平面 Cremona 映射的二次长度取值由是否 de Jonquières 决定，范围为 2 至 5，其中 de Jonquières 型可达到上界 5，且此类达到上界的映射已被分类。

Abstract: Every non-linear plane Cremona map can be decomposed into quadratic maps, and the minimum number of quadratic maps required is called its quadratic length. It is known that plane Cremona maps of degree 3 have quadratic length either 2 or 3.
  In this paper, we study the quadratic length of plane Cremona maps $\varphi$ of degree 4. Recall that either $\varphi$ is de Jonquières, i.e. it has a base point of multiplicity 3, or it is not de Jonquières. In the latter case, $\varphi$ has quadratic length 2, whereas in the former case we prove that $\varphi$ has quadratic length 3, 4, or 5, and we classify those maps that reach the upper bound.

</details>


### [367] [A rational surface with discrete and non-finitely generated automorphism group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40045)
*Tien-Cuong Dinh,Keiji Oguiso,Xun Yu,Qi Zhou*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 构造了一个光滑复射影有理曲面，其自同构群离散且非有限生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自同构群通常具有丰富的结构，此前缺少离散且非有限生成的自同构群的光滑复射影有理曲面实例。本文旨在给出这一反例性质的构造。

Method: 对乘积Kummer曲面的有理商的分歧曲线上一点作爆破（blowup）构造曲面；证明中运用分歧曲线上的首项射流（leading jets）、爆破的完全族（complete family of blowups）以及该族的立方相交（cubic intersection）。

Result: 得到了光滑复射影有理曲面，其自同构群离散且非有限生成；且对具有超越坐标的任意点，相应的爆破曲面均具备该性质。

Conclusion: 首次构造出此类具有离散且非有限生成自同构群的光滑复射影有理曲面，所用方法为相关早期工作未曾考虑的。

Abstract: We construct a smooth complex projective rational surface whose automorphism group is discrete and not finitely generated. It is obtained by blowing up a point on a branch curve of a rational quotient of a product Kummer surface. Every point with transcendental coordinate gives such a surface. The proof uses leading jets along the branch curve, the complete family of blowups and the cubic intersection of the family, which are not being considered in relevant earlier works.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [368] [CraftSPH: A high-accuracy and composable differentiable SPH solver implemented in PyTorch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38208)
*Gen Matono,Shujiro Fujioka,Mayuko Nishio*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出 CraftSPH，一个高精度、可组合的可微 SPH 求解器，将主要计算操作模块化，支持多种数值格式与自动微分，可用于正/反问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPH 适合大变形流体问题，近年 SPH 公式与可微求解器均有进展，但能灵活兼容多样数值格式（含高级与隐式方法）并支持持续扩展更新的统一框架仍有限。

Method: 将主要计算操作实现为独立模块，按求解目的组合构建求解器；支持不同计算格式由通用组件组合而成，便于纳入新开发的高精度离散方法；各模块支持自动微分，可与深度学习模型结合用于参数估计与优化。

Result: 正问题方面，对 Poiseuille 流、二维与三维溃坝问题及上升气泡问题进行了前向分析；反问题方面，通过 Taylor-Green 涡与顶盖驱动方腔流的参数估计评估了自动微分性能。

Conclusion: CraftSPH 以统一且可扩展的方式集成了高精度离散、显式与隐式计算及自动微分，证明其可适用于广泛问题。

Abstract: Smoothed Particle Hydrodynamics (SPH) is well suited to a range of problems, particularly those involving large deformations in fluid dynamics. In recent years, in addition to the advancement of SPH formulations, the development of differentiable solvers has also progressed. However, unified frameworks that flexibly accommodate diverse numerical schemes, including advanced and implicit methods, while supporting continuous extension and updating remain limited. In this study, a high-accuracy and composable differentiable SPH solver, CraftSPH, has been developed. In CraftSPH, major computational operations are implemented as independent modules, which can be combined according to the intended purpose of constructing a solver. This design enables different computational schemes to be intuitively constructed from combinations of common components and allows newly developed high-accuracy discretization methods to be flexibly incorporated and extended. Furthermore, each module supports automatic differentiation, enabling applications to physical-parameter estimation and to optimization that combines SPH solvers with deep learning models. Accordingly, CraftSPH provides an SPH computational framework in which high-accuracy discretization, explicit and implicit computations, and automatic differentiation can be handled in a unified and extensible manner. To demonstrate the applicability of CraftSPH to a wide range of problems, forward analyses are conducted for Poiseuille flow, two- and three-dimensional dam-break problems, and a rising-bubble problem. In addition, the performance of automatic differentiation is evaluated through inverse problems involving parameter estimation for the Taylor-Green vortex and lid-driven cavity flow.

</details>


### [369] [Catheter-modulated transient solute dispersion in micropolar annular flow with a retentive and absorptive arterial wall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38423)
*Sohel Ahmed,Nanda Poddar,Kajal Kumar Mondal,Subham Dhar*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该研究将微极性流体在同心环隙（导管化动脉）中的瞬态溶质弥散问题，与Gill-Sankarasubramanian广义色散框架耦合，采用Rannacher阻尼Crank-Nicolson时间推进求解K0(t)、K1(t)、K2(t)，揭示微极性、管壁动力学与受限几何对溶质输运的独立与耦合影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导管化动脉中压力驱动微极性流的瞬态溶质色散涉及微极性、管壁反应动力学与几何受限的耦合，现有研究对短时色散主导机制及窄间隙下剪切色散抑制规律缺乏定量刻画。

Method: 采用精确稳态微极性速度场，耦合Gill-Sankarasubramanian广义色散框架，构建体相-表面层级方程，并用Rannacher阻尼Crank-Nicolson格式时间推进求解K0(t)、K1(t)、K2(t)；同时进行弱反应区和窄间隙（ε=1-λ）渐近分析。

Result: 交换系数与微极性参数无关，满足-K0(0+)=2(β+θDa)/(1-λ²)；初期K2-Pe⁻²∼σv²t，短时色散由流体力学主导。弱反应区微极性对对流近似线性、对剪切色散二次修正。窄间隙给出v̄∼(2-Nc)ε²/6、K2-Pe⁻²∼(2-Nc)²ε⁶/7560，即六次幂抑制。λ由0.01增至0.30使有效对流降低约2.45倍、被动剪切色散降低24倍。并得到精确分配Φm+Φs+Φa=1。

Conclusion: 结果区分了受限与微极性的流体力学效应和管壁动力学效应，量化了反应调制的横向非均匀性，并给出可分离移动、可逆滞留与不可逆吸收溶质的精确分配关系。

Abstract: We study transient solute dispersion in pressure-driven micropolar flow through a concentric annulus representing a catheterized artery. The catheter is impermeable to solute, whereas the arterial wall combines irreversible removal with reversible surface retention. The exact steady micropolar velocity field is coupled to the Gill-Sankarasubramanian generalized-dispersion framework; the resulting bulk-surface hierarchy is advanced with Rannacher-damped Crank-Nicolson time stepping to determine $K_0(t)$, $K_1(t)$ and $K_2(t)$. The exchange coefficient is independent of the micropolar parameters and satisfies $-K_0(0^+)=2(β+θDa)/(1-λ^2)$, whereas $K_2-Pe^{-2}\simσ_v^2t$ initially, so the leading short-time dispersion is hydrodynamic and independent of wall kinetics. In the weak-reaction regime, micropolarity modifies convection approximately linearly and shear dispersion quadratically through the velocity-amplitude factor. A narrow-gap analysis with $\varepsilon=1-λ$ gives $\bar v\sim(2-N_c)\varepsilon^2/6$ and $K_2-Pe^{-2}\sim(2-N_c)^2\varepsilon^6/7560$ for a fixed pressure gradient, revealing a sixth-power suppression of shear dispersion as the catheter approaches the arterial wall. Increasing $λ$ from $0.01$ to $0.30$ reduces effective convection by about a factor of $2.45$, while chemically passive shear-induced dispersion falls by a factor of $24$. The coupled formulation also yields the exact partition $Φ_m+Φ_s+Φ_a=1$, separating mobile, reversibly retained and irreversibly absorbed solute. The reconstructed field quantifies reaction-modulated transverse non-uniformity, while the results distinguish hydrodynamic effects of confinement and microrotation from kinetic wall effects.

</details>


### [370] [Exact Periodic Solutions of the Forced Incompressible Navier-Stokes Equations in Arbitrary Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38210)
*R. K. Michael Thambynayagam*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文构造了强迫不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程在任意维度n≥3下的精确解：取n参数族两项循环场的横向部分U⁰作为体积力，得到封闭解；对3≤n≤8证明U⁰对任意相位向量非零，且该强迫不做净功，动能纯黏性衰减，不受雷诺数限制，可作为任意维度的精确基准解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无强迫不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程的精确周期解要求对流场为纯梯度（可被压力吸收），对循环三角族而言仅在孤立相位配置下成立，且仅存在于三维和四维。作者希望突破这一限制，构造适用范围更广（任意相位、任意维度）的精确解。

Method: 针对互补的强迫问题，考虑n参数族两项循环场，该场为无散度且为Laplacian本征函数；取其横向部分U⁰作为体积力，得到强迫方程的封闭形式解。对3≤n≤8证明U⁰对任意相位向量均非零，从而保证强迫非平凡。分析强迫的做功性质与动能演化。

Result: 对每个相位向量和每个维度n≥3，强迫问题均存在精确解；3≤n≤8时强迫恒非平凡。强迫不做净功，动能仅因黏性衰减，与雷诺数无关。速度、压力、体积力均显式给出。此外，在零功约束下研究涡量集中，分辨良好的零功计算显示在考察的雷诺数范围内峰值涡量放大随雷诺数增加，并表明表观饱和可能源于空间分辨率不足。

Conclusion: 该构造提供了任意维度下可用的精确基准解，且速度、压力与体积力全部显式；零功约束下的涡量集中研究表明，峰值涡量放大随雷诺数增大，表观饱和可由分辨率不足导致，提示数值研究中需注意空间分辨率对结果的影响。

Abstract: Exact periodic solutions of the unforced incompressible Navier-Stokes equations require the convective field to be a pure gradient, so that it can be absorbed into the pressure. For the cyclic trigonometric families considered previously, this occurs only at isolated phase assignments and only in three and four dimensions.
  We show that the complementary forced problem admits an exact construction for every phase vector and every dimension n>=3. For an n-parameter family of two-term cyclic fields that is solenoidal and a Laplacian eigenfunction, taking the transverse part U^0 of the convective field as the body force gives a closed-form solution of the forced equations; for 3 <= n <= 8 we establish that U^0 is non-zero for every phase vector, so that the forcing is never trivial. The forcing does no net work, so the kinetic energy decays purely viscously, with no restriction on the Reynolds number. The construction is therefore available as an exact benchmark in arbitrary dimension, with velocity, pressure, and body force all explicit.
  We also use the same construction to study vorticity concentration under a zero-work constraint. The resolved, zero-work computations show increasing peak-vorticity amplification over the Reynolds-number range examined and demonstrate that apparent saturation can result from insufficient spatial resolution.

</details>


### [371] [Airfoil2Vec: Spectral Geometry-Conditioned Neural Surrogate Models for Airfoil Aerodynamics and a Downforce-Generating CFD Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38213)
*Haitz Sáez de Ocáriz Borde,Flavio Savarino,Andrei Cristian Popescu,Pietro Innocenzi,Pantelis Papageorgiou,Xerxes Xian Chong*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该论文构建了约1万个翼型RANS仿真数据集，提出Airfoil2Vec谱几何编码器，并系统比较多种神经代理模型，实现快速且泛化的流场预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对汽车与赛车空气动力学中下压力翼型快速流场预测需求，传统CFD计算代价高昂，需评估并改进几何条件神经代理模型的泛化能力。

Method: 构建约10,000个稳态、不可压、二维亚声速NACA 4位数翼型RANS仿真数据集；比较神经场与神经ODE、MLP与图模型、多种谱几何条件方法；提出Airfoil2Vec编码器，融合联合轮廓谱与弯度、厚度的分离谱表示。

Result: 代理模型能准确捕捉气动量与定性流动特征，在攻角插值、未见NACA几何插值/外推及未见非NACA翼型上评估泛化，并较传统CFD实现数量级加速。

Conclusion: 该数据集与Airfoil2Vec为下压力翼型快速流场预测提供了有效方案，兼顾精度与泛化，显著优于传统CFD速度。

Abstract: We introduce a dataset of approximately 10,000 Reynolds-Averaged Navier-Stokes (RANS) simulations of steady, incompressible, two-dimensional subsonic flow around downforce-generating NACA 4-digit airfoils, targeting aerodynamic regimes relevant to automotive and motorsport applications (openly available on https://huggingface.co/datasets/ratiolabs/downforce-airfoils). Using this resource, we study geometry-conditioned neural surrogates for fast flow prediction, comparing neural fields with neural ODEs, MLPs with graph-based models, and several spectral geometry-conditioning methods. We further propose Airfoil2Vec, an airfoil-specific spectral geometry encoder that combines the joint contour spectrum with separate spectral representations of camber and thickness, for predicting continuous pressure and velocity fields. We evaluate generalization through angle-of-attack interpolation, interpolation and extrapolation to unseen NACA 4-digit geometries, and generalization to unseen non-NACA airfoils. The resulting surrogate accurately captures aerodynamic quantities and qualitative flow features while providing orders-of-magnitude speedups over conventional computational fluid dynamics.

</details>


### [372] [Three-Dimensional Simulation of Flood Propagation at Syabrubesi, Nepal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38351)
*Rainald Löhner,Harbir Antil*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 采用不可压缩自由表面模型对尼泊尔Syabrubesi处Bhote Koshi--Langtang Khola交汇口的三维洪水传播进行数值模拟，基于非结构四面体网格求解Navier--Stokes方程与界面输运方程，揭示弯道与河道宽度变化下的水流加速、展布与减速特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于一次实际洪水事件，旨在理解交汇口复杂地形（弯道与河道宽度突变）对洪水传播过程的影响，为当地洪水风险评估提供依据。

Method: 构建基于事件前地形数据的非结构四面体网格；求解不可压缩Navier--Stokes方程与自由表面界面输运方程；设定入口流速为10 m/s，模拟水流通过交汇口的传播过程。

Result: 入口流速10 m/s条件下，模拟得到表面流速超过40 m/s；洪水前沿向交汇口区域展布；随流动发展，弯道处水深增大、流速降低；而上游与下游束窄区段保持较高流速。

Conclusion: 该三维自由表面模型能够刻画交汇口地形突变下的洪水传播与流速再分布特征，表明弯道壅水减速与束窄段持续高速并存，可为类似山区河流交汇口的洪水分析提供参考。

Abstract: Three-dimensional flood propagation through the Bhote Koshi--Langtang Khola junction at Syabrubesi, Nepal, is studied using an incompressible free-surface model. The calculation is motivated by the August 2026 flood and examines the passage of an imposed inflow through the local bends and changes in channel width. The Navier--Stokes equations and an interface transport equation are solved on unstructured tetrahedral meshes constructed from pre-event terrain data. For an inlet speed of $10\,\mathrm{m\,s^{-1}}$, the solution indicates surface speeds above $40\,\mathrm{m\,s^{-1}}$. The advancing front spreads across the junction; as the flow develops, water deepens and slows around the bend. Higher speeds persist in the confined upstream and downstream regions.

</details>


### [373] [A macroscopic-shadow-corrected lattice Boltzmann method for fast, time-accurate simulation of low-Reynolds-number transient flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39474)
*Yanhui Ding,Yaolong Yu,Yuan Yu,Hao Zhang*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出宏观阴影修正格子玻尔兹曼方法(MSC-LBM),通过对未分裂动力学残差施加缺陷修正并结合精确的每波数Stokes型求解,突破了声学时间步限制,在N=256、Re=1时实现最高52.71倍墙钟加速,并随网格细化单调提升至N=1024的122.02倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式格子玻尔兹曼模拟慢瞬态流动受声学时间步约束;双时间步进虽可解除该限制,但内层收敛慢,墙钟增益仅约4–10倍,且缺乏随网格细化而增强的机制。

Method: 提出MSC-LBM,对未分裂动力学残差进行缺陷修正;在每个Fourier波数上对守恒残差矩精确求解类Stokes系统,同时保持BDF2不动点与时间精度。利用双松弛时间轴Λ=1/4、ω+=ω-=1使单次碰撞消除所有非流体动力学扰动,并在ν=1/6处令η(ν)=|6ν-1|/(6ν+1)消失,仅保留可由修正器反演的流体动力学慢模态长波阴影。三维扩展为D3Q19,并采用反弹一致的动力学粗求解器与自适应重线性化处理全封闭腔体。

Result: 计时实验中,N=256、Re=1时在匹配精度与1%误差门限下分别获得27.95倍与52.71倍墙钟加速;匹配加速比随网格细化单调上升,至N=1024达122.02倍,且在Re=10^-4至100范围内增益持续。三维D3Q19在N=128、Re=1下同样门限内达27.85倍与8.84倍。全封闭腔体扩展在N=128下,排除设置开销的物理推进加速比为Re=10时26.86倍、Re=100时3.84倍。精确的每波数符号逆确定了可达到的非设计收缩包络。

Conclusion: 收缩、参数扫描与计时结果共同界定了MSC-LBM的适用区间:低至中等雷诺数的瞬态流动,声学步进不再主导计算成本。

Abstract: Explicit lattice Boltzmann simulations of slow transient flows are constrained by the acoustic time step. Dual-time stepping can remove this restriction, but slow inner convergence has limited reported wall-clock gains to roughly four- to tenfold, without a mechanism that strengthens under refinement. We present the macroscopic-shadow-corrected lattice Boltzmann method (MSC-LBM), which applies defect correction to the unsplit kinetic residual. At each Fourier wavenumber, a Stokes-like system is solved exactly for the conserved residual moments while preserving the second-order backward-differentiation formula (BDF2) fixed point and temporal accuracy. Along the two-relaxation-time axis $Λ=1/4$, $ω^+=ω^-=1$ makes one collision eliminate all non-hydrodynamic perturbations; $η(ν)=|6ν-1|/(6ν+1)$ vanishes at $ν=1/6$, leaving hydrodynamic slow modes whose long-wave shadow is inverted by the corrector. In completed timing campaigns, MSC-LBM achieves 27.95- and 52.71-fold wall-clock speedups at $N=256$, $\mathrm{Re}=1$ under matched-accuracy and $1\%$ gates. The matched speedup rises monotonically to 122.02 at $N=1024$, with gains persisting across $\mathrm{Re}=10^{-4}$--$100$. The three-dimensional D3Q19 extension reaches 27.85 and 8.84 under the same gates at $N=128$, $\mathrm{Re}=1$. A bounce-back-consistent kinetic coarse solver with adaptive relinearisation extends MSC-LBM to fully enclosed cavities, yielding setup-excluding physical-march speedups of 26.86 at $\mathrm{Re}=10$ and 3.84 at $\mathrm{Re}=100$ for $N=128$. An exact per-wavenumber symbol inverse establishes the attainable off-design contraction envelope. The contraction, parameter-sweep, and timing results jointly delimit the demonstrated operating regime: low-to-moderate-Reynolds-number transients for which acoustic stepping need not dictate computational cost.

</details>


### [374] [Generalised Mixing-Plane Method for Compressible Reacting-Mixture Flows in Steady Multiphysics Turbomachinery Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39746)
*Yifeng Wang,Lin Shi,Fenglai Huang,Rui Wang,Feng Wang,Hui Xu*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文将经典混合平面法扩展到可压缩反应流，采用嵌套算法求解非线性热力学封闭，实现全发动机多物理场涡轮机械仿真的守恒且热力学一致的界面耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全发动机仿真需同时耦合压气机、涡轮与燃烧室，但燃烧后工质因组分变化与燃烧产物不能再视为完全气体，基于固定组分气体的传统混合平面法无法保证热力学一致的混合出口状态。

Method: 扩展经典混合平面方法以处理可压缩反应流；用嵌套算法处理热力学封闭的非线性，外层为压力根搜索、内层为焓反演；加入物理状态检查与幂律压力采样，提高低法向速度下压力根接近物理上界时的数值鲁棒性。

Result: 在三个算例中验证：准一维界面测试揭示不同燃料与流动条件下所提混合平面格式的热力学刚性；Darmstadt跨声速压气机算例证实当混合分数趋于零时方法退化为经典混合平面公式；KJ66微型涡喷全发动机仿真展示多物理场性能。结果与实验数据吻合良好，混合平面处最大相对质量流误差低于0.11%。

Conclusion: 该方法为可压缩反应涡轮机械流动提供了守恒且热力学一致的界面耦合能力，适用于全发动机多物理场仿真。

Abstract: The mixing-plane method plays a pivotal role in steady simulations of multiple turbomachinery components. With advances in computational power, a whole-engine gas turbine simulation is no longer an elusive approach. However, whole-engine simulations require simultaneous coupling of the compressor, turbine and combustor. A key challenge is that the working fluid after the combustor can no longer be treated as a perfect gas, since the combustion introduces composition variations and combustion products. Conventional mixing-plane methods based on a fixed-composition gas cannot guarantee a thermodynamically consistent mixed-out state. To overcome the difficulty, this paper extends the classic mixing-plane approach to handle compressible reacting flows. The nonlinearity in the thermodynamic closure is addressed using a novel nested algorithm combining an outer pressure-root search with an inner enthalpy inversion. Physical state checks and power-law pressure sampling are incorporated to improve numerical robustness at low normal velocities where the pressure root approaches its upper physical bound. The performance of the method is demonstrated in three configurations: a quasi-1D interface test elucidating the thermodynamic stiffness of the proposed mixing-plane formulation for different fuel types and flow conditions; the Darmstadt transonic compressor case that verifies the proposed method reduces to the classic mixing-plane formulation when the mixture fraction reduces to zero; and a whole-engine simulation of the KJ66 micro-turbojet is used to demonstrate the performance of the method for multiphysics turbomachinery simulations. Good agreement with experimental data is observed, and the maximum relative mass-flow error across the mixing planes remains below 0.11%, demonstrating conservative and thermodynamically consistent interface coupling for compressible reacting turbomachinery flows.

</details>


### [375] [Stable and Interpretable Multi-Mode Rheological Universal Differential Equations (mmRUDEs) for Data-Driven Constitutive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38470)
*Mohua Das,Nicholas King,Navid Azizan,Gareth H. McKinley*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出稳定性与可靠性导向的多模态RUDE框架，通过对数压缩、分裂网络与可微投影层解决热力学容许性、分布外预测与可解释性问题，并在合成与实验数据上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始RUDE框架将神经网络嵌入框架无关的张量本构主干，虽能数据驱动发现复杂流变行为，但其灵活性带来部署困难：学习到的神经网络修正会使本构方程在训练中数值刚性化、将修正分散到非唯一的张量基组合中，并随非线性增强而外推性能差，对具有宽弛豫谱的工业相关材料尤为突出。

Method: 开发多模态RUDE框架：对输入不变量进行对数压缩，采用分裂网络架构改善训练中的数值条件；引入可微投影层在每个时间步强制满足Clausius–Duhem不等式；并使用合成数据（多模态Giesekus模型）和缠结硅聚合物熔体的实验数据进行验证；同时提出"rheome"表示以解释各学习修正的贡献。

Result: 两个模型均在LAOS下训练，在未见的流动条件下保持稳定和准确，包括稳态剪切、瞬态应力增长和拉伸流动；而无约束模型在这些条件下可能出现数值发散。rheome能够紧凑表示主导的加权张量基贡献。

Conclusion: 对RUDE框架的这些改进产生了稳定且可解释的训练模型，为计算流体动力学模拟中的实现以及软材料加工操作的理性设计奠定基础。

Abstract: The Rheological Universal Differential Equation (RUDE) framework embeds neural networks within a frame-indifferent tensorial constitutive backbone and so enables data-driven discovery of complex material rheological behavior. However, the flexibility that makes the RUDE framework attractive also makes it difficult to deploy: the learned neural network correction can make the constitutive equation numerically stiff during training, distribute corrections across non-unique combinations of tensor-basis terms, and extrapolate poorly with increasing nonlinearity, especially for industrially relevant materials with broad relaxation spectra. We develop a stability- and reliability-focused multi-mode RUDE framework that addresses three key challenges: thermodynamic admissibility, out-of-distribution prediction, and interpretability. Logarithmic compression of the input invariants and a split-network architecture improve numerical conditioning during training, while a differentiable projection layer enforces the Clausius--Duhem inequality at every time step. We illustrate the framework with both synthetic (a multi-mode Giesekus model) and experimental data on an entangled silicone polymer melt. Both models were trained on LAOS; they remain stable and accurate under unseen flow conditions, including steady shear, transient stress growth, and extensional flows, for which the unconstrained models may diverge numerically. To aid in interpretation of the contributions of each learned correction to the rheological behavior, we introduce the rheome, a compact way of representing the dominant weighted tensor-basis contributions to the overall rheological behavior. These improvements to the RUDE framework lead to stable and interpretable trained models, paving the way for implementation in computational fluid dynamics simulations and for guiding the rational design of soft material processing operations.

</details>


### [376] [Directional Control of Droplet Motion via Geometric Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38654)
*Manoj K. Chaudhury,Vanessa Elias,Michelle Fruge*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该论文探索在PDMS等弹性体基底上驱动水滴运动的新方法，提出曲面PDMS平台引导液滴沿测地线运动，以及在PDMS上构建二极管式门控实现单向运动，并组合两种策略实现单/多液滴的定向操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有利用硅油溶胀的PDMS基底使水滴易移动，但通过浅开放通道和陷阱控制液滴轨迹的方法需要沿两个正交方向倾斜基底，难以同时操控多个液滴，因此需要开发新的液滴操控方法。

Method: 方法一：使用曲率半径远大于液滴尺寸和移动距离的平凹PDMS平台，通过改变系统构型引导液滴沿弯曲测地线运动。方法二：在交联前将薄刚性板嵌入PDMS（平面或平凹膜），再经硅油溶胀形成S形台阶，构成类似二极管的门控，限制液滴单向运动。最后将两种策略结合使用。

Result: 平凹PDMS平台可在系统构型改变时稳定地引导液滴沿弯曲测地线运动；嵌入刚性板并溶胀后形成的S形台阶门控可将液滴运动限制在单一方向；两种策略结合可实现单液滴和多液滴的多种定向运动形式。

Conclusion: 通过在弹性体基底上引入曲面几何与二极管式门控结构，可实现液滴的定向及多液滴操控，为软材料表面的液滴操控提供了新思路。

Abstract: We explore new methods for inducing the movement of water droplets on elastomeric substrates, such as polydimethylsiloxane (PDMS). Our approach builds upon the well-known phenomenon that water droplets move easily across these substrates when swelled w,ith silicone oil. While designing shallow open channels and traps on a PDMS film offers a simple way to control a droplet's trajectory by tilting the substrate in two orthogonal directions, this method is poorly suited for manipulating multiple droplets simultaneously. To address this limitation, we introduce two new methods. The first method utilizes a plano-concave PDMS platform with a radius of curvature significantly larger than the size of the droplet and its translation distance. When the system's configuration is altered, this curved substrate consistently guides the droplet along a curved geodesic. The second method incorporates diode-like gates on the PDMS. These gates are created on either flat or plano-concave PDMS films by embedding thin, rigid plates into the elastomer prior to crosslinking, followed by swelling in silicone oil. This process generates sigmoidal steps that restrict droplet motion to a single direction. Finally, we demonstrate how combining these two strategies enables various forms of directed motion for both single and multiple droplets.

</details>


### [377] [A Reynolds-like Criterion for the Onset of Universal Active Turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39999)
*Kunal Kumar,Sandip Sahoo,Kirti Kashyap,Amal Manoharan,Siddhartha Mukherjee,Anupam Gupta,Samriddhi Sankar Ray*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出一个类雷诺数的无量纲控制参数，统一刻画活性湍流普适态（含$E(k)\sim k^{-3/2}$能谱）的起始阈值，并预测不同参数路径下的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性湍流在临界Toner-Tu驱动之上呈现参数无关的普适统计（如$E(k)\sim k^{-3/2}$能谱）。然而，达到该普适态的途径是否唯一、是否存在统一的控制量尚不清楚。

Method: 识别支配动力学的独立无量纲群，构建类雷诺数控制参数；利用已知的活性驱动控制转变进行标定，并在其他参数路径（增强非线性对流、减弱非线性饱和、调节Swift-Hohenberg不稳定性）上验证普适态的起始。

Result: 发现同一普适态可由多种路径达到；单一临界值能够预测其他参数路径下普适性的起始，并将各路径的转变坍缩到共同阈值上。

Conclusion: 研究识别出普适活性湍流起始的动力学组织参数，并为Toner-Tu-Swift-Hohenberg普适类提供了一个类雷诺数度量。

Abstract: Beyond a critical Toner--Tu drive, bacterial turbulence exhibits universal statistics, including a parameter-independent energy spectrum $E(k)\sim k^{-3/2}$. We show that the same universal state can be reached not only by increasing activity, but also by strengthening nonlinear advection, weakening nonlinear saturation, or tuning the Swift--Hohenberg instability. Motivated by the competition between these processes, we identify the independent dimensionless groups governing the dynamics and construct a Reynolds-like control parameter from them. A single critical value, calibrated from the known activity-controlled transition, predicts the onset of universality along the other parameter routes and collapses them onto a common threshold. These results identify a dynamical organizing parameter for the onset of universal active turbulence and provide a Reynolds-like measure for the Toner--Tu--Swift--Hohenberg universality class.

</details>


### [378] [Separated flows over swept wings across transitional Reynolds numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38732)
*Laura Victoria Rolandi,Jonathan Quang Tran,Kunihiko Taira*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过参数化数值研究，揭示了有限翼后掠角与雷诺数对前缘分离与翼尖涡相互作用的调控机制，发现后掠角在低雷诺数下降低升力、在高雷诺数下提升升力的转折规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对低雷诺数（约10^2）下后掠有限翼分离流已有较多探讨，但对高雷诺数（约10^4）湍流工况的理解不足，二者之间存在知识空白。本文旨在桥接这一差距，并阐明后掠角与雷诺数如何共同影响流场结构与气动性能，为现代飞行器设计提供依据。

Method: 针对半展弦比sAR=2的有限翼开展参数化研究，后掠角Λ从0°变化至45°，攻角固定为14°，弦长雷诺数Re取600、1000、2500、5000和10000。通过分析流向涡量等流动量，考察不同参数组合下的流场结构演变及其对升力特性的影响。

Result: 流场结构随参数显著变化，进而改变升力特性：Re≤5000时增大Λ降低升力，Re>5000时增大Λ提升升力。流向涡量分析揭示三个关键效应：(i) 随后掠角增大，展向速度分量增强导致流向涡量符号改变；(ii) 后掠角增大使翼尖涡逐渐减弱乃至消失，同时在翼根附近出现占主导的内侧涡结构；(iii) 在高雷诺数和大后掠角下，该内侧涡结构与主尾迹融合，强化剪切层卷起，形成具有扩展再附区的前缘涡。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了流动特性随雷诺数的演化规律：从后掠翼收益有限的流态过渡到显著提升气动性能的流态。该工作深化了对后掠翼尾迹动力学的理解，对现代飞行器设计具有重要意义。

Abstract: We explore the effect of wing sweep on the interaction between leading-edge separation and tip vortex for a finite wing with sweep angles $Λ=0^\circ$ to $45^\circ$, semi-aspect-ratio $sAR=2$, angle of attack $14^\circ$, and chord-based Reynolds numbers $Re=600$, $1000$, $2500$, $5000$, and $10000$. Through this parametric study, we seek to bridge the knowledge gap between existing studies on separated flows over swept finite wings at low Reynolds numbers ($Re\approx 10^2$) and the behavior of turbulent flows at higher Reynolds numbers ($Re\approx10^4$). The flow structure significantly varies over this range of parameters, altering the lift characteristics.
