Specifically, increasing $Λ$ at $Re\leq5000$ reduces the lift, while increasing $Λ$ at $Re>5000$ enhances the lift. The flow modifications that lead to this effect with increasing Reynolds number are analyzed in terms of the streamwise vorticity, revealing three key effects: (i) a change in the sign of streamwise vorticity with increasing sweep angle, due to the higher spanwise velocity component, (ii) the progressive weakening and eventual disappearance of the tip vortex, when increasing the wing sweep, is accompanied by the emergence of a dominant inboard vortical structure near the root, and (iii) at higher Reynolds numbers and sweep angles, this inboard vortical structure merges with the main wake, intensifying the shear-layer roll-up and giving rise to a leading-edge vortex with an extended reattached region over the wing. These findings reveal how the flow characteristics vary with Reynolds number, transitioning from regimes where swept wings offer limited benefit to regimes where they significantly enhance the aerodynamic performance. This study contributes to a deeper understanding of swept-wing wake dynamics, which is crucial for modern air vehicle design.

</details>


### [379] [Imperfect bifurcations in a laminar 3D bluff body wake: effect of pitch and yaw](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39364)
*Atharva Lagwankar,Alessandro Chiarini,François Gallaire,Edouard Boujo*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文用三维计算研究俯仰与偏航对Ahmed体层流尾迹分岔的影响，揭示小攻角下二次分支失稳、以及偏航/俯仰分别经不同Hopf与叉式分岔竞争导致丰富分岔图与多稳态解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完美对齐来流的Ahmed体尾迹先经叉式分岔产生静态垂向或水平偏转，再经次级Hopf分岔变为振荡。实际中任何微小错位（imperfect pitchfork）会预先选定某一稳态（"主"分支），并在较大Re下经鞍结分岔产生不连通的"次"分支。作者希望系统理解俯仰(pitch)与偏航(yaw)错位如何改变这些分岔结构及稳定性。

Method: 针对W/H=1.2、L/H=3的Ahmed体进行三维数值计算：先对纯俯仰和纯偏航情形做线性稳定性分析；再用直接数值模拟(DNS)研究垂直Hopf与水平叉式分岔之间的非线性竞争；最后用弱非线性分析研究俯仰与偏航同时存在时两个静态模态之间的竞争。

Result: 纯俯仰与纯偏航下，一旦入射角超过很小值，次分支即失稳。纯偏航时主分支经水平Hopf分岔失稳（被偏航去稳）。纯俯仰时主分支出现垂直Hopf与水平叉式分岔的交叉，分别被俯仰去稳与被俯仰稳定。俯仰与偏航同时存在时，两静态模态的竞争产生丰富分岔图。

Conclusion: 弱非线性分析可预测稳定解的数量及不同状态之间的相空间轨迹，说明俯仰与偏航的联合错位是决定Ahmed体尾迹分岔拓扑与稳定性选择的关键因素。

Abstract: We study the effect of pitch and yaw on the bifurcations of the laminar flow past an Ahmed body, a bluff body of width-to-height ratio $W/H=1.2$ and length-to-height ratio $L/H=3$. When perfectly aligned with incoming flow, its wake first undergoes pitchfork bifurcations leading to a static vertical or horizontal deflection, and becomes oscillatory via a secondary Hopf bifurcation at larger Reynolds number $Re$. As commonly observed for imperfect pitchfork bifurcations, any small misalignment preselects one of the steady states, referred to as the "primary" branch, at low $Re$. At larger $Re$, a disconnected "secondary" branch is born via a saddle-node bifurcation. Here, we investigate the effect of misalignment using three-dimensional computations. We first focus on pure pitch and pure yaw with linear stability analysis. In both cases, the secondary branch becomes unstable as soon as the incidence exceeds a small value. In the pure yaw case, the primary branch becomes unstable via a horizontal Hopf bifurcation, destabilised by yaw. In the pure pitch case, the primary branch sees the crossover of vertical Hopf and horizontal pitchfork bifurcations, destabilised and stabilised by pitch, respectively. This motivates use of DNS to study this nonlinear competition, already observed for aligned Ahmed bodies in ground proximity. We then study the effect of simultaneous yaw and pitch with a weakly nonlinear analysis. The competition between the two stationary modes gives rise to rich bifurcation diagrams, allowing us to predict the number of stable solutions and phase space trajectories between different states

</details>


### [380] [Rigorous vertical averaging for pseudo-3D modeling of precipitation obstructed fluid flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39391)
*David Krach,Felix Weinhardt,Holger Class,Holger Steeb,Martin Schneider*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于垂直平均的伪三维Stokes求解器，用于计算孔隙形态随时间与空间变化的孔隙介质的固有渗透率k(x,t)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态变化孔隙形态下的有效水力特性计算在理论与应用科学中复杂且关键，尤其需要低成本地处理大尺度、强非均质域，而现有方法在成本与精度间难以兼顾。

Method: 利用碳酸钙沉淀微流控实验的瞬时快照样本；从零推导含垂直平均阻力项的垂直平均控制方程且不引入限制性假设；采用附加粘性阻力项的伪三维Stokes求解器以合理成本计算大域；对矩形通道内不同尺寸半球形沉淀物，比较多种阻力公式的建模方法，并与三维求解器对比精度与计算效率；最终计算完整实验域并给出渗透率张量k。

Result: 在示例沉淀物及EICP实验片段中，垂直平均阻力项在速度与精度上均为最优方案，与三维求解器结果吻合良好；完整实验域的渗透率张量k计算结果与实验结果可比较，能够高效分析孔隙空间改变引起的局部流动非均匀性。

Conclusion: 所提伪三维、带垂直平均阻力项的求解器在精度与效率之间取得最优平衡，可有效计算时变孔隙结构下的渗透率场并支持实验尺度分析。

Abstract: Computing effective hydraulic properties of porous materials with a dynamically changing pore morphology presents a complex and critical challenge in both theoretical and applied sciences. This paper presents a tool to effectively compute the intrinsic permeability k(x,t) for domains with both temporally and spatially varying pore structures, by utilizing a sample of snapshots of microfluidic experiments with calcium carbonate precipitation. To compute large domains at reasonable cost, a pseudo-3D Stokes solver with an additional viscous drag term is employed. In order to simulate the intricate domains with highly heterogeneous pore space, we derive vertically averaged governing equations including a vertically averaged drag term from scratch and without limiting assumptions. For exemplary precipitates (semi-spheres) of varying sizes within a rectangular channel with fixed solid boundaries, results are compared across multiple modeling approaches, each employing different drag force formulations. In addition, the performance of the derived models is examined for segments of an EICP experiment in terms of accuracy (comparison with 3D solvers) and computational efficiency. Finally, an entire experimental domain is used to demonstrate the capabilities offered by the solver thanks to its efficiency as well as accuracy and the permeability tensor k is computed and compared with experimental results. In all these applications, the vertically averaged drag term proves to be the optimal solution in terms of speed and accuracy, thus allowing for an efficient analysis of the non-uniformity of local fluid flow fluctuations resulting from alterations in the pore space.

</details>


### [381] [Coupled Rigid-Body Motion and Binary Phase Change: A Sharp Level-Set/Embedded-Boundary Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39431)
*Chao-Fan Zhang,Zhong-Han Xue,Jie Zhang*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文提出一种用于自由运动固体颗粒纯熔化和二元熔化的锐利界面方法，通过Level-Set保持几何、嵌入边界施加界面条件、动切割单元保守离散温度与浓度，并迭代耦合流体载荷、刚体运动与相变，验证并应用于浮冰圆盘等强耦合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由运动固体颗粒的相变涉及流体动力、传热传质与刚体运动的强耦合，且长时间平移旋转下几何保真度、标量守恒与固相温度输运等问题难以兼顾，现有方法难以锐利、保守地处理此类问题。

Method: 采用Level-Set表征颗粒几何以保持长距离平移与旋转下的形状；用嵌入边界格式在欧拉笛卡尔网格上锐利施加流体动力、热与溶质条件；液相温度与溶质浓度用保守动切割单元离散推进，固相温度通过几何一致的保守示踪剂输运；每个物理时间步内迭代耦合流体载荷、刚体运动与相变。

Result: 方法经刚体运动、规定运动下纯熔化与二元熔化、以及自由运动全耦合熔化逐步验证，复现了已有参考解；捕捉到熔化球的尾流诱导旋转，以及三维扁椭球的同时平移、旋转与热溶质熔化；浮冰圆盘模拟揭示热对流不稳定、几何约束与熔化共同决定其自发运动，相变通过演化几何反馈作用于不稳定；附加测试证实长时间几何保真、局部标量守恒与几何一致固相温度输运的重要性。

Conclusion: 该框架为强耦合运动体相变问题提供了锐利且保守的求解途径。

Abstract: We develop a sharp-interface method for freely moving solid particles undergoing pure or binary melting. A Level-Set representation preserves the particle geometry during long-distance translation and rotation, while an embedded-boundary formulation imposes hydrodynamic, thermal and solutal conditions sharply on a Eulerian Cartesian mesh. Liquid temperature and solutal concentration are advanced with a conservative moving-cut-cell discretization, whereas the solid temperature is transported through a geometry-consistent conservative tracer. Hydrodynamic loads, rigid-body motion and phase change are coupled iteratively within each physical time step. The method is validated progressively for rigid-body motion, prescribed-motion pure and binary melting, and fully coupled melting with free particle motion. It reproduces established reference solutions and captures wake-induced rotation of a melting sphere and the simultaneous translation, rotation and thermosolutal melting of a three-dimensional oblate ellipsoid. As a final application, simulations of a floating ice disc reveal how thermoconvective instability, geometric confinement and melting jointly determine its spontaneous motion, with phase change feeding back on the instability through the evolving particle geometry. Additional tests establish the importance of long-time geometrical fidelity, local scalar conservation and geometry-consistent solid-temperature transport. The resulting framework provides a sharp and conservative approach for strongly coupled moving-body phase-change problems.

</details>


### [382] [Two-way actuator-line coupling of a geometrically exact finite-volume beam with incompressible and free-surface flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39443)
*Amirhossein Taran,Seevani Bali,Željko Tuković,Vikram Pakrashi,Philip Cardiff*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出一种有限体积致动线方法，将几何精确Simo–Reissner梁与不可压缩有限体积流动求解器双向耦合，用于细长柔性结构的流固耦合，并在OpenFOAM中公开实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系泊缆、养殖网衣、水生植被等细长柔性结构过细，采用贴体网格经济性差，但又与流动发生双向动量交换，因此需要一种无需贴体网格的高效流固耦合建模方法。

Method: 采用基于单元中心有限体积离散的几何精确Simo–Reissner梁，通过守恒且并行一致的传递算子与不可压缩有限体积流动求解器双向耦合；在每个梁控制体上游采样流体速度，用准稳态Morison型阻力闭合计算线力，并通过归一化高斯核将等大反向反作用力投影到流体动量方程以守恒力；两子系统共享离散、时间循环与区域分解，无需外部结构代码。

Result: 在稳态受限通道流中，壁面悬臂梁端部挠度落在已发表连续介质解范围内；在正弦通道流的柔性瓣片中，较长强迫周期下峰值端部位移偏离参考解，因为瓣片相对流体运动更大，被阻力闭合忽略的附加质量力与历史力变得重要；在波浪水槽中，模拟茎干再现了实测模态、波内不对称性与底部受力，并恢复71–83%的振荡范围；准静态重构测试表明剩余偏差源于省略的流体惯性而非结构模型。

Conclusion: 该致动线公式仅凭预先固定的阻力闭合即可在多种基准中含理地预测细长柔性结构的流固耦合响应，其适用范围受限于附加质量与历史力可忽略的条件，偏差主要来自省略的流体惯性效应。

Abstract: Slender flexible structures such as mooring lines, aquaculture netting, and aquatic vegetation are too thin to be resolved economically by a body-fitted mesh, yet they exchange momentum with the flow in both directions. This paper presents a finite volume actuator-line formulation for the fluid--structure interaction of such structures, in which a geometrically exact Simo--Reissner beam, discretised by cell-centred finite volumes, is coupled two-way to an incompressible finite volume flow solver through conservative, parallel-consistent transfer operators. The fluid velocity is sampled upstream of each beam control volume, a quasi-steady Morison-type drag closure evaluates the line force, and the equal and opposite reaction is projected into the fluid momentum equation through a normalised Gaussian kernel that conserves force. The two subsystems share a discretisation, time loop, and domain decomposition, so no external structural code is required, and the OpenFOAM implementation is released publicly. The accuracy of the drag-only closure and the conditions over which it holds are quantified against two channel benchmarks and a wave-flume experiment, with all drag coefficients fixed in advance. In a steady confined channel flow the tip deflection of a wall-mounted cantilever falls within the range of published continuum solutions. For a flexible leaflet in sinusoidal channel flow the peak tip excursion departs from the reference at the longer forcing period. The leaflet then moves further relative to the surrounding fluid, so added-mass and history forces, which the closure omits, become important. In the wave flume the simulated stem reproduces the measured mode shape, intra-wave asymmetry, and base force, and recovers 71--83% of the oscillation range. A quasi-static reconstruction test places the remaining shortfall in omitted fluid inertia rather than in the structural model.

</details>


### [383] [Role of flame localization in wavemaker regions on the suppression of combustion instability in turbulent partially-premixed methane flames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39502)
*Abhishek Kushwaha,Bjarne Lieth,Isaac Boxx*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究通过低动量二次燃料喷射与燃料分级实验，验证了火焰与波源区（wavemaker region）空间重叠是旋流稳定燃烧器稳定性的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火焰动力学与水动力不稳定性之间的相互作用对旋流稳定燃烧器的稳定性起决定作用，但火焰稳定是否依赖其与波源区的空间重叠尚缺乏直接的实验证据。

Method: 在旋流稳定燃烧器中心体上周向开孔引入低动量二次甲烷喷射，喷射速度低于主流速度的5\%以减小对流场的动量扰动，并采用燃料分级从主燃料供给中分流等量燃料，从而将火焰位置迁移效应与总燃料流量效应解耦；结合线性稳定性分析确定波源区位置。

Result: 二次喷射使火焰由M型转变为V型，燃料分级产生相似响应，表明该现象主要源于释热再分布；线性稳定性分析显示波源区仍位于燃烧室入口附近，火焰根部由抬升的M型火焰转变为附着的V型火焰，并稳定在波源区对应的径向位置。

Conclusion: 稳定工况下火焰附着位置与波源区位置的重合提供了有力实验证据，表明火焰稳定由二者的空间重叠而非底层水动力不稳定性的改变所主导；该框架为理解火焰稳定与水动力不稳定性的耦合提供了新见解，并为稳定低排放燃烧系统设计提供了实用指导。

Abstract: The interaction between flame dynamics & hydrodynamic instabilities plays a fundamental role in determining the stability of swirl-stabilized combustors. In the present study, we investigate the hypothesis that spatial overlap between flame & wavemaker region is a necessary prerequisite for combustion stability in flows characterized by a wavemaker region. To test the hypothesis, a low-momentum secondary methane injection was introduced through circumferential holes located on the centerbody of a swirl-stabilized burner. The injection velocity was maintained below 5\% of bulk flow velocity to minimize momentum-induced modifications of flow field while selectively redistributing heat release. In addition, an equivalent amount of fuel was diverted from primary fuel supply to secondary injection ports, known as fuel-staging, to isolate the effects of flame relocation from those of the total fuel flow rate. The introduction of secondary injection produced a transition of the flame from M-shaped to V-shaped structure, while fuel-staging yielded a similar flame response, demonstrating that observed behavior results primarily from redistribution of heat release. Linear stability analysis revealed that wavemaker region remains near inlet of the combustion chamber. Moreover, flame root transitions from a lifted M-flame to attached V-flame and consistently stabilizes at radial position corresponding to the identified wavemaker region. The coincidence of flame attachment & wavemaker location under stable operating conditions provides strong experimental evidence that flame stabilization is governed by their spatial overlap rather than by a modification of the underlying hydrodynamic instability. The proposed framework provides new insight into the coupling between flame stabilization & hydrodynamic instability & offers practical guidance for the design of stable, low-emission combustion systems.

</details>


### [384] [Is Vorticity Amplification Essential for Anomalous Dissipation and Intermittency in Turbulence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39826)
*Alessandro Chiarini*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 通过修正Navier–Stokes方程选择性抑制涡量放大（VA）、保留涡量倾斜（VT）的直接数值模拟，揭示VA是维持经典正向能量级串和能量耗散反常的必要机制；完全抑制VA后系统转向纯正向拟涡能级串，出现拟涡能耗散反常与E(k)~k^{-3}谱，但多重分形间歇性仍由VT单独维持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维均匀各向同性湍流中，涡量放大（VA）与涡量倾斜（VT）在动力学上具有不同作用，但二者对能量级串、耗散反常及间歇性的具体贡献尚缺乏机制层面的分离认识。作者希望厘清哪一机制真正支配小尺度梯度增强与极端涨落，以及间歇性是否依赖于VA。

Method: 采用修正Navier–Stokes系统的直接数值模拟，在保留VT的同时选择性地逐步抑制VA（直至完全抑制），并比较螺旋与非螺旋大尺度强迫；通过尺度逐尺度（scale-by-scale）能流/拟涡能收支分析和标度律拟合，考察能量耗散、拟涡能耗散、能谱及多重分形谱的变化。

Result: 逐步抑制VA会削弱小尺度速度梯度、显著削减极端涨落，并完全消除经典能量耗散反常，使归一化能量耗散按Re_λ^{-1}衰减；完全抑制VA时拟涡能成为相关无粘不变量，动力学转为纯正向拟涡能级串，表现为拟涡能耗散反常和稳健的E(k)~k^{-3}中间能谱；拟涡能反常耗散存在Re_λ^{-1/2}的有限雷诺数修正；这些渐近标度极限与强迫是否螺旋无关。尽管速度场被平滑且能量耗散反常消失，反常结构标度和宽多重分形谱仍然持续存在。

Conclusion: VA与VT在机制上存在根本分离：VA负责放大强烈的局域涨落并维持能量耗散反常与经典正向能量级串，而仅由VT实现的几何重排足以机械地维持多重分形间歇性；完全抑制VA后湍流可由能量级串转为拟涡能级串而仍保持间歇性。

Abstract: The distinct dynamical roles of vorticity amplification (VA) and vortex tilting (VT) in three-dimensional homogeneous isotropic turbulence are investigated using direct numerical simulations of a modified Navier--Stokes system. By selectively suppressing VA while retaining VT, we demonstrate that gradient amplification by VA is strictly required to sustain the classical forward energy cascade. Progressively suppressing VA weakens small-scale velocity gradients, heavily depletes extreme fluctuations, and entirely eliminates the classical energy dissipative anomaly, causing the normalized energy dissipation to decay as $Re_λ^{-1}$. In the limit of complete VA suppression, enstrophy emerges as the relevant inviscid invariant. Scale-by-scale budget analyses confirm that the dynamics transition to a purely forward enstrophy cascade, characterized by an anomalous enstrophy dissipation and a robust $E(k) \sim k^{-3}$ intermediate energy spectrum. We verify that these asymptotic scaling limits---including a $Re_λ^{-1/2}$ finite-Reynolds-number correction for the enstrophy anomalous dissipation---are universal properties of the VA-suppressed dynamics, independent of whether the large-scale forcing is helical or non-helical. Remarkably, despite the smoothing of the velocity field and the elimination of the energy dissipative anomaly, anomalous structural scaling and a broad multifractal spectrum persist. These results reveal a fundamental mechanistic separation: while VA is responsible for amplifying intense localized fluctuations, geometric reorganization by VT alone is mechanically sufficient to sustain multifractal intermittency.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [385] [Robust LassoNet: Enhancing Feature Selection in Neural Networks via Robust Loss Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38263)
*Daniela De Canditiis,Italia De Feis,Paola Stolfi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 Robust LassoNet，将 Huber、Cauchy、Tukey bisquare 与 Nonnegative Garrote 等鲁棒损失函数引入 LassoNet 框架，以缓解离群点对神经网络特征选择的干扰，在保持原优化结构的同时提升污染数据下的预测与特征选择性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络中的特征选择在噪声或受污染数据下仍具挑战性。LassoNet 通过层级稀疏约束实现预测与变量选择同步进行，但其标准形式依赖 MSE 损失，对离群点高度敏感，因此需要在保留原框架的前提下引入鲁棒性。

Method: 提出 Robust LassoNet：在 LassoNet 的层级稀疏约束优化框架下，替换 MSE 为多种鲁棒损失函数，包括 Huber、Cauchy、Tukey's bisquare 以及 Nonnegative Garrote，从而在数据污染情形下抑制极端观测的影响，同时保留原有优化结构。

Result: 在合成数据集与真实数据集上的实验表明，当存在离群点或重尾噪声时，Robust LassoNet 在预测性能和特征选择准确率上均有显著提升；在干净数据场景下性能与标准 LassoNet 相当。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性对基于神经网络的特征选择至关重要。Robust LassoNet 兼顾预测与变量选择，并针对不同损失函数给出了实用的选择建议。

Abstract: Feature selection in neural networks remains a challenging problem, particularly in the presence of noisy or contaminated data. LassoNet is a recent approach that addresses this issue by combining neural networks with hierarchical sparsity constraints, enabling simultaneous prediction and variable selection. However, its standard formulation relies on the mean squared error (MSE) loss, which is known to be highly sensitive to outliers. In this paper, we present Robust LassoNet, an extension of LassoNet that incorporates robust loss functions, such as Huber, Cauchy, Tukey's bisquare, and Nonnegative Garrote, to mitigate the effect of extreme observations. The proposed approach preserves the original optimization framework while improving stability under data contamination. Through experiments on synthetic and real datasets, we show that robust LassoNet significantly improves both predictive performance and feature selection accuracy in the presence of outliers or heavy tailed noise, while maintaining comparable performance in clean settings. These results highlight the importance of robustness in neural network based feature selection and suggest practical guidelines for choosing appropriate loss functions.

</details>


### [386] [Acceleration of Diffusion Language Model through Discrete Average Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38364)
*Yidong Ouyang,Zhengyan Wan,Themis Haris,Tian Tan,Liqian Peng,Henry Li,Ziqian Lin,Jianhang Chen,Maryam Karimzadehgan,Alec Go,George Michailidis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文提出离散平均生成器（Discrete Average Generator），将MeanFlow思想推广到连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMCs），实现离散扩散与流匹配的少步高效生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型与流匹配在离散状态空间生成建模中表现强大，但高效少步生成仍是根本性挑战。

Method: 类比MeanFlow在连续空间定义时间区间上的平均速度场，论文将平均生成器定义为时间区间上转移核归一化增量，证明其满足自洽恒等式作为训练目标基础；设计契合扩散语言模型标准训练范式的训练策略并保持目标可解；在逐坐标边缘投影下自洽恒等式具有闭式表达，支持高效训练与推理。

Result: Potts模型模拟中，K步采样器的总变差距离最多降低67%。OpenWebText上，8至64步采样时取得所评估方法中最低的生成困惑度，并实现16倍加速；ImageNet上性能与现有方法相当。

Conclusion: 该工作为CTMCs上的少步生成提供了原理性框架，验证了离散平均生成器在离散状态空间高效生成中的有效性。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models and flow matching have emerged as powerful frameworks for generative modeling over discrete state spaces, yet efficient few-step generation remains a fundamental challenge. In this work, we introduce the Discrete Average Generator, a principled extension of MeanFlow to Continuous-Time Markov Chains (CTMCs). Analogously to how MeanFlow defines an average velocity field over a time interval in continuous spaces, we define an average generator as the normalized increment of the transition kernel over a time interval. We show that this average generator satisfies a self-consistency identity, which provides the foundation for our training objective. We further develop training strategies that align with the standard training paradigm of diffusion language models while keeping the resulting objective tractable. When projected onto per-coordinate marginals, the self-consistency identity admits a closed-form expression, enabling efficient training and inference. In Potts model simulations, our objective reduces the total variation distance of the $K$-step sampler by up to 67%. On OpenWebText, our method achieves the lowest generative perplexity among the evaluated methods for 8 to 64 sampling steps while enabling a $16\times$ acceleration, and achieves comparable performance to existing methods on ImageNet.

</details>


### [387] [Lower Bounds for Linear-Oracle Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38375)
*Mohit Sinha*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在仅依赖一阶线性最小化预言机的确定性学习模型中，每轮常数次线性最小化无法突破在线 Frank-Wolfe 的 $T^{3/4}$ 遗憾率，并给出显式下界构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线 Frank-Wolfe 在一般凸集上的遗憾上界为 $T^{3/4}$，但能否通过每轮进行常数次线性最小化来改进该速率尚不明确。Weibel 等人猜想固定系数方法无法改进，本文旨在证明该猜想并将其推广到更一般的确定性学习器。

Method: 在 oracle-only 模型下定义确定性学习器：给定初始可行点与直径界，每次决策之间最多调用 $b$ 次线性最小化预言机，且需在每个与预言机回复一致的域上保持可行。构造维度 $d=2b(T-1)+1$ 的对抗实例，对抗者在博弈前固定域、初始点、确定性打破平局规则与线性损失；顶点构成一条路径，使决策前可见点当前损失为零，而末端顶点每轮损失为负。

Result: 对于 $T$ 轮、每轮最多 $b$ 次调用、直径界 $D$、梯度范数界 $L$，构造实例的遗憾至少为 $2^{-1/4}LDb^{-1/4}T^{3/4}$。当 $b$ 为常数时，该下界与已知的维度无关上界匹配。对具有最新梯度非零系数的单次调用固定调度，另一构造给出遗憾至少 $3LDT^{3/4}/4$，且每次查询的唯一极小点。对 Weibel 等人的调优调度的精确算术证书与其有限时域数值最坏情况高度吻合，且预言机回复唯一。

Conclusion: 论文证明了 Weibel 等人的猜想：固定系数方法无法改进 $T^{3/4}$ 遗憾率，并将下界扩展到 oracle-only 模型中的任意确定性学习器，表明每轮常数次线性最小化在一般凸集上无法优于 $T^{3/4}$，同时为相关调优调度提供了精确算术验证。

Abstract: Can a constant number of linear minimizations per round improve on the $T^{3/4}$ regret rate of online Frank-Wolfe on general convex sets? Weibel et al. conjectured that fixed-coefficient methods cannot. We prove their conjecture and extend the lower bound to every deterministic learner in an oracle-only model. The learner receives an initial feasible point and a diameter bound, and must remain feasible on every domain consistent with its oracle replies. For $T$ rounds, at most $b$ calls between decisions, diameter bound $D$, and gradient norm bound $L$, we construct an instance in dimension $d=2b(T-1)+1$ with regret at least $2^{-1/4}LDb^{-1/4}T^{3/4}$. The adversary fixes the domain, initial point, deterministic tie rule and linear losses before play. The vertices form a path on which every point available before a decision has zero current loss, while the final vertex has negative loss on every round. For constant $b$, the result matches the known upper rate for dimension-independent guarantees. For one-call fixed schedules with a nonzero coefficient on the newest gradient, a second construction gives regret at least $3LDT^{3/4}/4$ with unique minimizers at every issued query. Exact-arithmetic certificates for the tuned schedule of Weibel et al. closely match their finite-horizon numerical worst cases, with unique oracle replies.

</details>


### [388] [Generative sequence modeling for infinite memory processes via predictive states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38524)
*Michael Wieck-Sosa,Cosma Rohilla Shalizi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出基于预测状态（predictive states）的多变量随机过程一步条件分布估计方法，可处理无限长记忆，其收敛速度由预测状态空间的内在维度决定，并给出深度神经网络实例的理论保证与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多依赖有限记忆范围、稀疏性或可加性等假设，难以适用于具有长程非线性交互的过程；而放弃这些结构假设后，非参数估计又会因维数灾难而困难。

Method: 引入基于过程预测状态（可含无限长记忆）的估计框架：当历史可压缩为对未来预测充分的低维统计量时，估计的统计复杂度由预测状态空间的内在维度而非原始历史维度决定；并基于深度神经网络估计器实现该方法。

Result: 证明了当历史可压缩为低维充分统计量时，估计器达到快速收敛速率，且统计复杂度由预测状态空间的内在维度决定；同时为基于深度神经网络的实现建立了理论保证，并通过实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 通过预测状态建模，可在不依赖有限记忆、稀疏性或可加性假设的前提下有效估计长程非线性过程的预测分布，为高维非参数估计提供了一种由内在维度控制复杂度的新途径。

Abstract: We consider estimating the one-step-ahead conditional distribution of a multivariate stochastic process. Many existing approaches rely on assumptions such as finite-range memory, sparsity, or additivity, which can be poorly suited to processes with long-range nonlinear interactions. However, without such structural assumptions, nonparametric estimation is challenging due to the curse of dimensionality. To address this challenge, we introduce a new estimation approach based on the predictive states of a process, possibly with infinite-range memory. We show that our estimator achieves fast convergence rates when the past history can be compressed into a low-dimensional statistic that is sufficient for predicting the future. Specifically, we show that the statistical complexity of the estimation problem is determined by the intrinsic dimension of the predictive state space. We establish guarantees for an instantiation of our method based on deep neural network estimators, and we support these theoretical results with experiments.

</details>


### [389] [Bandits with Multiple Optimal Arms: Minimax Regret and Non-Adaptivit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38659)
*Kaixuan Ji,Qiwei Di,Qingyue Zhao,Heyang Zhao,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究具有多个最优臂的多臂老虎机问题，给出子采样算法的更精细分析，建立 $\tilde{O}\big(\frac{K-A}{\sqrt{KA}}\sqrt{T}\big)$ 的极小极大遗憾，并证明匹配下界及 $A$ 知识的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际决策问题存在多个正确答案，即多臂老虎机中可能存在多个最优臂，而现有理论多假设唯一最优臂，对多最优臂情形的遗憾刻画不够精确。

Method: 针对 $K$ 臂、$A$ 个最优臂的多臂老虎机，对已有子采样算法（De Heide et al., 2021; Zhu and Nowak, 2020）进行更精细的遗憾分析，并构造匹配的下界，同时分析最优臂数量 $A$ 的知识对遗憾的影响。

Result: 证明子采样算法达到 $\tilde{O}\big(\frac{K-A}{\sqrt{KA}}\sqrt{T}\big)$ 的极小极大遗憾，优于此前 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{KT/A})$；给出至多相差对数因子的匹配下界，说明该速率近似极小极大最优；并表明需已知 $A$ 到 $\tilde{O}(1)$ 因子才能达到近最优遗憾。

Conclusion: 结果在 $1\le A\le K-1$ 全范围内完整刻画了具有 $A$ 个最优臂的 $K$ 臂老虎机的极小极大遗憾，证明新速率近似最优且对 $A$ 的近似知识是必要的。

Abstract: We study multi-armed bandits (MAB) with multiple optimal arms, motivated by the fact that many practical decision making problems admit multiple correct answers. For $K$-armed bandits with $A$ optimal arms, we first provide a sharper analysis of previous sub-sampling algorithms (De Heide et al., 2021; Zhu and Nowak, 2020), establishing a $\tilde{O}\Big(\frac{K-A}{\sqrt{KA}}\sqrt{T} \Big)$ minimax regret, where $T$ is the total number of interactions and $\tilde O(\cdot)$ drops all constant and logarithmic factors, improving the previous $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{KT/A})$ regret. We then provide a matching lower bound up to logarithmic factors, indicating that our established rate is nearly minimax-optimal. We further show that the knowledge of $A$ up to $\tilde{O}(1)$ factors is necessary to achieve near-optimal regret, as near-optimal algorithms for one number of optimal arms must incur substantially larger regret than optimal regret for a smaller number. Overall, our results provide a comprehensive minimax characterization of $K$-armed bandits with $A$ over the entire range of $1 \leq A \leq K-1$.

</details>


### [390] [Sharp Statistical Rates for Asynchronous TD Learning with Markovian Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38880)
*Yang Peng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文证明了标准表格型时序差分（TD）学习在单条轨迹下最后迭代的收敛速率：以高概率达到ε精度的转移数为 Õ(H³/(μ_min ε²) + t_mix/μ_min)，同时适用于常数步长和递减步长，并给出了匹配的极小极大下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究单条轨迹下标准表格型TD学习最后迭代的收敛性，旨在得到无额外混合时间因子的紧收敛速率，并刻画统计项与混合暂态的精确依赖关系。

Method: 采用逆向时间下的锚定局部泊松方程控制随机波动（避免混合时间因子），利用首达时间补偿恒等式约束初始化误差，并通过期望累积传播质量的上界将论证推广至递减步长。

Result: 最后迭代TD以高概率达到 sup 范数误差不超过ε，所需转移数为 Õ(H³/(μ_min ε²) + t_mix/μ_min)（0<ε≤1）；统计项保持同步TD的三次有效时域依赖，附加混合暂态不含额外时域因子；结果允许非可逆链、任意初始分布及有界奖励。三状态构造给出统计项与混合项（至对数因子）匹配的极小极大下界。

Conclusion: 该工作为单轨迹TD最后迭代给出了近乎最优的收敛保证，既适用于常数步长也适用于与目标精度和终止时间无关的递减步长（对超过显式暂态阈值的所有时刻同时成立），并建立了匹配的极小极大下界。

Abstract: We study the last iterate of standard tabular temporal-difference (TD) learning from a single trajectory of a finite Markov reward process. For discount factor $γ$, write $H=(1-γ)^{-1}$, and let $μ_{\min}$ and $t_{\operatorname{mix}}$ denote the minimum stationary probability and total-variation mixing time. We prove that last-iterate TD achieves sup-norm error at most $\varepsilon$ with high probability using$\widetilde O\left(
  \frac{H^3}{μ_{\min}\varepsilon^2}
  +\frac{t_{\operatorname{mix}}}{μ_{\min}} \right)$ transitions, for $0<\varepsilon\leq1$. This rate holds both for a constant step size selected for the target accuracy and for a decreasing schedule independent of the target accuracy and terminal time. The latter gives a simultaneous guarantee over all times beyond an explicit transient threshold. The statistical term retains the cubic effective-horizon dependence of synchronous TD, and the additive mixing transient has no extra horizon factor. The result allows non-reversible chains, arbitrary initial state distributions, and bounded rewards that may depend on the next state. The proof uses an anchored local Poisson equation in reverse time to control stochastic fluctuations without a mixing-time factor, and a hitting-time compensation identity to bound initialization error. The latter also yields a finer transient in terms of the worst expected reverse hitting time. A bound on the expected cumulative propagation mass extends this argument to decreasing step sizes. A three-state construction with known deterministic rewards gives matching minimax lower bounds for the statistical and mixing terms, up to logarithms, over specified model classes in a slow-mixing parameter regime.

</details>


### [391] [Warm-starting PDE solvers with any-dimensional machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38916)
*Wilson G. Gregory,George A. Kevrekidis,Ben Blum-Smith,Soledad Villar*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明：当PDE及其初值具有特定对称性时，低维训练的PDE学习求解器可零样本迁移到高维PDE；若仅方程满足对称性而数据不满足，则可据此为高维求解器提供有原则的热启动。在热方程、Burgers方程和可压缩Navier–Stokes方程上，该方法在高维数据上同时改善了零样本与常规训练的表现，例如用2D数据训练的代理模型在3D测试上优于用3D数据训练的基线，且仅用12% FLOPs和20%数据量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机来自图神经网络（GNN）的迁移性研究：任意维度的机器学习模型可自然处理不同尺寸与维度的输入。作者希望将这种"低维训练、高维推理"的能力推广到基于PDE学习的求解器，以缓解高维PDE数据获取与训练成本高昂的问题。

Method: 方法核心是给出数学条件：当PDE本身与初始数据同时满足某种对称性时，低维训练的求解器可直接零样本应用于更高维PDE。当方程满足对称性但数据不满足（物理中常见情形）时，该理论提供一种为高维PDE求解器进行热启动（warm-start）的原则性方式。作者将该方法应用于热方程、Burgers方程和可压缩Navier–Stokes方程进行验证。

Result: 在热方程、Burgers方程和可压缩Navier–Stokes方程上，方法在高维数据的零样本和常规训练两种情形下均提升了性能。典型案例：仅用2D Navier–Stokes数据训练代理模型，在3D测试数据上超过用3D数据训练的基线模型，同时仅消耗12%的FLOPs和20%的总数据量。

Conclusion: 该工作为低维PDE求解器向高维PDE的迁移提供了理论依据与实用手段，既能实现零样本维度泛化，也能作为高维训练的有效热启动策略，显著降低计算与数据成本。

Abstract: Any-dimensional machine learning models, such as graph neural networks (GNNs), can be naturally trained and evaluated on inputs of different sizes and dimensions. Inspired by the GNN transferability literature, we show mathematical conditions under which a partial differential equation (PDE) learning-based solver can be trained in small dimensions and directly applied to solve a higher dimensional PDE in a zero-shot fashion. These conditions are based on symmetries in both the partial differential equation and the initial data. When the equations satisfy the symmetries but the data does not, which is the case for many PDEs arising from physics, we show that our theory gives a principled way of warm-starting low-dimensional PDE solvers for higher dimensional PDEs. We apply this method on the heat equation, Burgers' equation, and the compressible Navier--Stokes equations, improving the performance in both zero-shot and typical training regimes on high dimensional data. For example, we train a surrogate model on 2D Navier--Stokes data and achieve better results on 3D test data than a baseline surrogate model trained on 3D data, while only using 12$\%$ of the flops and 20$\%$ of the total data size.

</details>


### [392] [Minimax rates for learning spectral Barron functions by deep ReLU neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39020)
*Songqiu Ma,Yunfei Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究深度ReLU网络对谱Barron函数的逼近与学习能力，给出了新的逼近速率并证明了学习速率的极小极大最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明浅层神经网络可高效逼近谱Barron函数类而不受维数灾难困扰，但缺乏关于深度网络的逼近界以及该类函数学习速率的极小极大最优性结论，本文旨在补全这两方面。

Method: 针对d维、光滑指数s>0的谱Barron函数，推导深度ReLU网络的逼近界：以网络非零参数数目S为度量，逼近速率为Õ(S^{-1/2-s/d})；在此基础上分析深度ReLU网络在n个训练样本下的学习收敛速率。

Result: 深度ReLU网络能以n^{-(d+2s)/(2d+2s)}的快速率学习谱Barron函数；并证明该收敛速率在忽略对数因子意义下是极小极大最优的。

Conclusion: 深度ReLU网络可高效逼近并学习谱Barron函数，其学习速率达到极小极大最优，表明深度网络在该函数类上兼具逼近效率与统计最优性。

Abstract: We study how well deep neural networks approximate and learn spectral Barron functions. Recent studies have shown that these function classes can be efficiently approximated by shallow neural networks without suffering from the curse of dimensionality. We complement these results by providing new approximation bounds for deep networks with ReLU activation and establishing the minimax rates for learning these function classes. Specifically, we show that $d$-dimensional spectral Barron functions with smoothness index $s>0$ can be approximated by deep ReLU neural networks with approximation rate $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}} (S^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{s}{d}})$, where $S$ denotes the number of nonzero parameters in the network. Using this approximation result, we further show that deep ReLU neural networks can learn spectral Barron functions in a fast rate $n^{-\frac{d+2s}{2d+2s}}$ with $n$ training samples. Finally, we prove that this convergence rate is minimax optimal up to logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [393] [Steepest Guidance: A Practical and Principled Approach to Inference-Time Alignment of Flow and Diffusion-based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39091)
*Shokichi Takakura,Akifumi Wachi,Rei Higuchi,Kohei Miyaguchi,Taiji Suzuki*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该文针对流/扩散模型推理时对齐问题，提出Steepest Guidance框架，将推理时对齐视为概率测度空间上的序贯优化问题，基于目标局部改进最大化原则进行引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时对齐流与扩散模型对灵活生成建模至关重要。理论上Doob h-变换提供优雅解法，且多数现有方法基于此，但实际中在推理时估计由Doob h-变换导出的最优引导十分困难。

Method: 将推理时对齐重新表述为概率测度空间中的序贯优化问题，提出名为Steepest Guidance的新框架，其核心原则是最大化目标的局部改进。

Result: 通过大量实验验证了所提方法的有效性，并给出了理论分析。

Conclusion: Steepest Guidance为推理时对齐提供了一种新视角与可行方案，在理论与实验上均展现出有效性。

Abstract: Inference-time alignment of flow and diffusion-based models is critical for achieving flexible generative modeling. Theoretically, Doob's $h$-transform provides an elegant solution to this problem, and most existing methods are based on this principle. However, in practice, estimating the optimal guidance derived from Doob's $h$-transform at inference time is challenging. To deal with this issue, we regard inference-time alignment as a sequential optimization problem in the space of probability measures and propose a novel framework called *Steepest Guidance*, based on the principle of maximizing local improvement in the objective. We provide a theoretical analysis of the proposed method and demonstrate its effectiveness through extensive experiments.

</details>


### [394] [Asymptotic Properties of Support Vector Machines in High-Dimension, Low-Sample-Size Settings under a Spiked Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39173)
*Yugo Nakayama*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究尖峰模型下高维低样本量（HDLSS）SVM的渐近性质，指出经典几何表示失效，SVM与BC-SVM均不具一致性，并提出尖峰校正SVM（SC-SVM）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HDLSS下SVM理论依赖几何表示，要求协方差矩阵特征值无主导成分；而在尖峰模型下该条件不成立，需要重新刻画SVM的渐近行为并寻找可行的一致性分类器。

Method: 在尖峰模型下分析HDLSS数据的Gram矩阵与经验协方差谱，推导其分布收敛到有限维随机构型；分析SVM与偏差校正SVM（BC-SVM）的误分类率；提出尖峰校正SVM（SC-SVM）并进行理论一致性与投影程序不可行性论证，最后用数值模拟验证。

Result: 几何表示在尖峰模型下不成立；HDLSS数据的Gram矩阵依分布收敛到随机矩阵，数据收敛到由尖峰个数决定的有限维随机构型；SVM误分类率不趋于零，BC-SVM因偏差项本身需修正而表现不佳；SC-SVM在大样本下具有一致性，且样本量增长是本质的，固定样本量时任何基于投影的方法都会失败。

Conclusion: 尖峰模型改变了HDLSS下SVM的渐近机制，经典与偏差校正方法均无法保证一致性；通过引入尖峰校正的SC-SVM可在样本量趋于无穷时实现一致性，数值模拟支持理论结论。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider asymptotic properties of the support vector machine (SVM) in high-dimension, low-sample-size (HDLSS) settings under a spiked model. The existing theory of the SVM in the HDLSS context relies on the geometric representation of HDLSS data, which requires that the eigenvalues of the covariance matrices are not dominant. We first show that the geometric representation does not hold under the spiked model. We show that the Gram matrix of HDLSS data converges in distribution to a random matrix, namely, the HDLSS data converge to a random configuration in a finite-dimensional space whose dimension is given by the number of the spikes. We show that the misclassification rates of the SVM do not tend to zero, that is, the SVM does not hold the consistency property. We also show that the bias-corrected SVM (BC-SVM) does not give preferable performance in this setting because the bias term itself should be modified. In order to overcome such difficulties, we propose a spike-corrected SVM (SC-SVM). We show that the SC-SVM holds the consistency property when the sample size goes to infinity, and that the growth of the sample size is essential in the sense that any projection-based procedure fails when the sample size is fixed. Finally, we check the performance of the classifiers by numerical simulations.

</details>


### [395] [Minimax Additive Regression under Unknown Dependent Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39212)
*Baptiste Ferrere,Fabrice Gamboa,Jean-Michel Loubes*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究 [0,1]^d 上允许非乘积随机设计、维度随样本量增长的加性回归，引入耦合光滑性类，利用函数型 ANOVA 的 Riesz 基构造与阈值最小二乘估计，得到预测的极小极大上下界，并刻画边界密度光滑度对收敛率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有加性回归理论多假定设计为乘积分布或有独立同分布结构，难以处理一般相依随机设计；且当维度随样本量增长时，密度光滑度与加性分量光滑度的耦合作用缺乏系统刻画。作者希望在此更一般的设定下建立自适应极小极大理论。

Method: 定义耦合光滑性类，分别控制边缘密度正则性与密度加权加性分量；针对相依性改造函数型 ANOVA 模型的 Riesz 基构造，在联合密度一致有界下建立常数与维度无关的兼容性界；构造阈值最小二乘估计量，分别处理边缘密度已知与未知两类情形。

Result: 在适当的维度增长条件下，建立了预测的匹配极小极大上下界。当边缘密度至少与加权分量同等光滑时，未知密度问题达到已知密度的极小极大率；当密度更不光滑时，其正则性决定耦合类上的极小极大率。此外，中心化加性分量可在同一聚合上界率下恢复，且不产生额外阶数误差。

Conclusion: 该工作将加性回归的极小极大理论推广至非乘积、相依且高维增长的设计场景，揭示了密度光滑度与分量光滑度的耦合机制，并通过 Riesz 基兼容性构造实现维度无关界，为未知设计下的高维加性建模提供理论支撑。

Abstract: We study additive regression under a potentially non-product random design on $[0,1]^d$, allowing the dimension $d$ to grow with the sample size $n$. We introduce coupled smoothness classes that separately control the regularity of the marginal densities and the density-weighted additive components. To handle dependence, we adapt a Riesz-basis construction for functional ANOVA models and establish compatibility bounds with constants independent of the dimension under uniform bounds on the joint density. We construct thresholded least-squares estimators and establish matching minimax upper and lower bounds for prediction with known or unknown marginal densities, under suitable dimension-growth conditions. When the marginal densities are at least as smooth as the weighted components, the unknown-density problem attains the known-density minimax rate. When the densities are less smooth, their regularity determines the minimax rate over the coupled class. Finally, we show that the centered additive components can be recovered at the same aggregate upper rate, without an additional order of error.

</details>


### [396] [Discrete Score Matching Enables Causal Discovery from Count Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39326)
*Euijong Song,Hyewon Park,Gunwoong Park*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向计数数据的因果发现框架：通过将SCORE的常曲率准则推广为条件曲率分(CCS)与off-diagonal曲率分(OCS)，并借助离散扩散实现DISCO算法，实现计数DAG的高效恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计数数据给基于score matching的因果发现带来困难：score函数涉及对变量的导数，而计数变量离散、导数不存在；简单用有限差分替代导数通常不足以支撑因果发现。因此需要一种能直接处理离散计数、并具备可识别性保证的新准则与算法。

Method: 1) 将SCORE的常曲率准则推广为对节点取值进行条件的条件曲率分CCS，用于因果排序；2) 扩展基于曲率的父节点恢复，提出off-diagonal曲率分OCS，实现DAG恢复；3) 对连续数据用score函数、对计数数据用concrete score构造两个分数；4) 理论分析：双变量下CCS为零恰好刻画一类半参数GLM条件形式（条件族无需预先指定）；在正则条件下，canonical-parameter非线性是双变量半参数GLM DAG可识别性的充要条件；多变量下该非线性使CCS与OCS可实现DAG恢复；5) 提出DISCO算法，用离散扩散估计CCS与OCS。

Result: 在Poisson、负二项、二项及混合族设置下均实现准确的DAG恢复；在单张GPU上可扩展到1,000节点DAG。理论上刻画了一类新的半参数GLM DAG，其严格包含SCORE识别的非线性高斯ANM类。

Conclusion: 该工作把曲率型因果发现从连续高斯/加性噪声情形推广到计数与半参数GLM情形，给出CCS/OCS可识别性理论与DISCO实用算法，为离散计数数据的因果结构学习提供了兼具理论保证与可扩展性的方案。

Abstract: Count data pose a challenge for score-matching-based causal discovery: derivatives are unavailable, and simply replacing them with finite differences does not generally suffice for causal discovery. We generalize SCORE's constant-curvature criterion (Rolland et al., 2022) by conditioning on the node's value, yielding the conditional curvature score (CCS) for ordering. We also extend curvature-based parent recovery through the off-diagonal curvature score (OCS), enabling directed acyclic graph (DAG) recovery with both scores constructed from score functions for continuous data and concrete scores for counts. In the bivariate setting, zero CCS exactly characterizes a semiparametric generalized linear model (GLM) conditional form in which the conditional family need not be specified in advance, unlike in classical GLMs. For bivariate semiparametric GLM DAGs under our regularity condition, canonical-parameter nonlinearity is necessary and sufficient for identifiability. In multivariate DAGs, this nonlinearity enables DAG recovery through CCS and OCS. Our framework identifies a new class of semiparametric GLM DAGs that strictly contains the nonlinear Gaussian ANM class identified by SCORE. We introduce DISCO (DIscrete SCOre), a count-DAG recovery algorithm that estimates CCS and OCS using discrete diffusion. Experiments demonstrate accurate DAG recovery across Poisson, negative binomial, binomial, and mixed-family settings, as well as scalability to 1,000-node DAGs on a single GPU.

</details>


### [397] [A Dynamical Theory of LoRA in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39367)
*Théo Marchetta,Filippo Alessandroni,Alessandro Breccia,Alessandro Ingrosso,Federica Gerace*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该文在可解的两任务教师-学生模型中对LoRA进行渐近精确的动力学刻画，导出了宏观序参量的常微分方程组，揭示了降低干扰与减慢适应之间的权衡，并提出状态依赖掩码策略以缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LoRA被广泛使用，但学界对其在持续学习中的动力学、以及低秩更新影响灾难性遗忘的机制仍缺乏理解，缺少精确的理论刻画。

Method: 在可解的两任务教师-学生模型中研究LoRA，于高维在线学习极限下推导有限个宏观序参量的封闭常微分方程组；基于机制分析提出状态依赖掩码策略，冻结承载最强第一任务表征的隐藏单元，仅在被掩码子空间上进行适配，并与有限维仿真及顺序MNIST基准对比。

Result: 理论给出初始任务1学习阶段与后续任务2 LoRA微调全过程的泛化误差精确表达式，与有限维仿真定量吻合；揭示LoRA低秩适配减少对第一任务特征的干扰但初始化会减慢对第二任务的适应。转移收益仅随秩提升至目标任务的内在维度，超过后饱和，而遗忘随秩持续增长；掩码策略显著降低遗忘并保持新任务可塑性，顺序MNIST上定性复现。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了低秩适配如何在序列任务间组织信息的动力学与几何解释，明确了秩与遗忘/转移的权衡关系，并为缓解持续学习中的灾难性遗忘提供了理论依据与可行方法。

Abstract: Despite the widespread use of Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), little is known about its dynamics in continual learning and the mechanisms by which low-rank updates affect catastrophic forgetting. We provide an asymptotically exact dynamical characterization of LoRA in a solvable two-task teacher-student model. In the high-dimensional online-learning limit, we derive a closed system of ordinary differential equations for a finite set of macroscopic order parameters, yielding exact expressions for the generalization errors throughout both the initial Task 1 learning phase and the subsequent LoRA fine-tuning on Task 2. The theory quantitatively matches finite-dimensional simulations and exposes two characteristic effects of LoRA: low-rank adaptation reduces interference with features learned on the first task, but its initialization slows adaptation to the second task. Building on this mechanistic picture, we analyze a state-dependent masking strategy that freezes hidden units carrying the strongest first-task representations and restricts adaptation to the complementary subspace. This structural partitioning markedly reduces forgetting, while preserving plasticity on the new task. Our framework further clarifies the role of adapter rank: transfer improves only up to the intrinsic dimensionality of the target task and saturates beyond it, while forgetting continues to grow with rank. These results provide a dynamical and geometric account of how low-rank adaptation organizes information across sequential tasks and are qualitatively reproduced on a sequential MNIST benchmark.

</details>


### [398] [Towards Optimal Inventory Control under Censored Demand: A Biased Sample-Average Approximation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39397)
*Yuxuan Han,Xiaoyu Fan,Jiawei Zhang,Zhengyuan Zhou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究删失需求下的数据驱动多周期缺货库存控制，提出基于成本分解与有偏样本平均近似（SAA）的统一策略学习框架，涵盖离线样本高效学习与在线近最优遗憾两类算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺货场景下需求被删失，观测仅显示需求超过库存水平，信息不完整，导致传统策略学习困难；需要明确何种条件下删失数据足以支撑样本高效的策略学习，并给出可操作的算法设计原则。

Method: 针对基准库存策略提出新的成本分解，并据此定义覆盖条件；在覆盖条件指导下设计两种有偏SAA算法：上偏算法用于离线场景，下偏算法通过主动生成所需覆盖用于在线场景。

Result: 上偏SAA在离线覆盖条件下达到近最优样本复杂度；下偏SAA在线实现近最优遗憾。有偏SAA为删失反馈下实现悲观与乐观提供了通用原则。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了删失数据下的库存策略学习问题，提出的覆盖条件与有偏SAA方法兼具理论保证与通用性，对删失反馈下的学习问题具有独立价值。

Abstract: We study data-driven multi-period lost-sales inventory control under censored demand, where a stockout reveals only that demand exceeded the stocking level. We develop a unified, model-based framework for policy learning from censored data, built on a new cost decomposition for base-stock policies and a biased sample-average approximation (SAA) approach. The cost decomposition allows us to propose a new coverage condition under which censored observations are informative enough for sample-efficient policy learning. Guided by this coverage condition, we design two biased SAA algorithms: an upper-biased one that achieves near-optimal sample complexity under the offline coverage condition, and a lower-biased one that actively generates the required coverage and achieves near-optimal regret online. More broadly, this biased SAA approach provides a general principle for implementing pessimism and optimism under censored feedback, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [399] [Principal Component Regression Dominates all Monotone Spectral Filters for Linear Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39440)
*Juno Kim,Hengyu Fu,Peter Bartlett,Jason D. Lee,Jingfeng Wu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文比较了线性回归中单调谱滤波器（含PCR、GD、岭回归）的逐实例有限样本风险，证明最优调参的PCR在所有问题上均不劣于任何单调谱滤波器（相差不超过常数因子），且当滤波器偏离阶跃函数时PCR可带来多项式级的显著优势，从而确立PCR在单调滤波器类中的最优性与容许性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单调谱滤波器（如PCR、GD、岭回归）是线性回归中的一大类估计器，但它们之间的逐实例有限样本风险比较尚不清晰。此前Wu等（2026）仅证明GD强支配岭回归，缺乏对更广类别内最优性的统一刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白并厘清各滤波器的相对优劣。

Method: 作者在逐实例、有限样本的框架下，对单调谱滤波器类的风险进行理论比较；针对一般谱滤波器建立新的风险上界与下界，并将其特化到岭回归与GD等具体滤波器，进而比较最优调参PCR与其他滤波器的风险关系。

Result: 最优调参PCR的风险对所有单调谱滤波器均不超过常数因子；当滤波器与阶跃函数分离（如GD、岭回归）时，存在问题实例使PCR的风险以样本量依赖的多项式因子更小；新建立的一般谱滤波器上下界在特化后可以恢复或改进已知最优界。

Conclusion: PCR在单调谱滤波器类中是最优且容许的，显著推广了GD强支配岭回归的已有结论，并为一般谱滤波器的风险分析提供了更紧的逐实例理论工具。

Abstract: We compare the instance-wise, finite-sample risks of monotone spectral filters for linear regression, a broad class of estimators including principal component regression (PCR), gradient descent (GD), and ridge regression. We show that PCR dominates all monotone spectral filters: compared to any such filter, the risk of optimally tuned PCR is no bigger by a constant factor for all problems. Furthermore, the dominance is strong if the filter is separated from step functions (e.g., GD and ridge): there exist problem instances for which the risk of PCR is smaller by a polynomial factor in sample size dependence. Our comparison results show that PCR is optimal and thus admissible among monotone filters, significantly extending Wu et al. (2026)'s result that GD strongly dominates ridge. From a technical perspective, we establish new upper and lower bounds for general spectral filters, which are instance-wise sharp when specialized to ridge or GD, recovering or improving the best-known bounds.

</details>


### [400] [Distributionally robust linear regression through the lens of adversarial training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39449)
*Elis Stefansson,David Vävinggren,Antônio H. Ribeiro*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文将 Wasserstein DRO 线性回归统一为涵盖平方根 Lasso 与对抗线性回归的一般方法，证明其具有非渐近误差界、对噪声水平不敏感（pivotal）及解等价性，并可高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wasserstein DRO 是分析鲁棒性与泛化性的原则性框架，且能推广多种正则化方法。平方根 Lasso 与对抗线性回归是其两个重要特例，但二者性质能否推广至一般 Wasserstein DRO 线性回归尚不明确，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 研究以 Wasserstein 距离构造分布不确定集的 DRO 线性回归；关键证明步骤是将该方法重写为二次型，从而模仿对抗线性回归的分析路径；并给出高效求解算法。

Result: 证明：(i) 确定性的非渐近样本内误差界，一般为 O(n^{-1/2})，在设计矩阵与稀疏性条件下为 O(n^{-1})；(ii) 对噪声水平不敏感，即 pivotal 性质；(iii) 小与大模糊集下的解等价性；并通过数值模拟验证结论。

Conclusion: Wasserstein DRO 线性回归统一并继承了平方根 Lasso 与对抗线性回归的诸多优良性质，兼具理论保证与计算可行性，数值实验支持理论发现。

Abstract: Distributionally robust optimization (DRO) studies parameter estimation under uncertainty in the underlying probability distribution and has emerged as a principled framework for analyzing robustness and generalization. In particular, Wasserstein DRO, with distributional uncertainty induced by the Wasserstein distance, generalizes several popular regularizers. This paper studies Wasserstein DRO linear regression, unifying square-root Lasso and adversarial linear regression as important special cases. We prove that many properties of these two special cases carry over to this general method. In particular, we show (i) deterministic and non-asymptotic in-sample error bounds $O(n^{-1/2})$ in general and $O(n^{-1})$ under design matrix and sparsity conditions; (ii) insensitivity to the noise level, also known as the pivotal property; and (iii) solution equivalences for small and large ambiguity sets. The key proof step is to recast the method into a quadratic form, mimicking adversarial linear regression. We also show that the method can be solved efficiently, and we validate our findings through numerical simulations.

</details>


### [401] [CAMOS: Coupled Oscillatory State-Space Model for Multimodal Clinical Time-Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39484)
*Maxx Richard Rahman,Mostafa Hammouda,Wolfgang Maass*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文指出现有线性状态空间模型通过掩码处理缺失模态存在表示能力缺陷，提出CAMOS模型，将模态可用性引入状态转移算子，并在ADNI等数据集上取得更优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向临床队列数据具有多模态、不规则采样且普遍缺失的特点（如ADNI中约一半随访缺少PET和CSF检测）。线性状态空间模型虽能自然处理不规则采样，但仅通过掩码处理缺失模态，未改变状态转移算子。论文旨在证明这种掩码方式的表示局限性，并提出能建模模态间联合可用性交互的方法。

Method: 论文证明：任何转移算子不依赖可用性模式的线性状态空间层，其隐状态都是可用性指示函数的加性函数，因此无法表示两个模态联合出现或联合缺失之间的交互。为此提出CAMOS：为每个模态配置二阶振荡器库，通过位于微分方程内部、由可用性门控的耦合矩阵连接，使转移算子成为实际测量模态的函数。为恢复耦合振荡模型的理论分析，作者引入逐通道Gershgorin预算，使有效刚度在所有2^M种可用性模式及任意间隔下一致正定；用能量论证将放大归因于可用性切换而非序列长度；并通过通道分解保持精确的结合式并行扫描。

Result: 在ADNI数据集上，CAMOS在同次随访分期、标志点预测和纵向预测任务上优于非耦合振荡状态空间模型及临床融合模型；在零样本迁移至OASIS-3时，CAMOS是唯一避免坍缩到多数类的模型。

Conclusion: CAMOS通过让状态转移算子依赖模态可用性并引入门控耦合，突破了线性状态空间模型在缺失模态建模上的表示限制，在临床多模态纵向任务中展现出更强的表达能力和迁移鲁棒性。

Abstract: Longitudinal clinical cohorts are multimodal, irregularly sampled and pervasively incomplete: in ADNI, positron emission tomography and cerebrospinal fluid assays are absent from roughly half of all visits. Linear state-space models handle irregular sampling gracefully but treat a missing modality by masking the input, leaving the transition operator untouched. We prove that this is a representational limitation: the latent state of any linear state-space layer whose transition operator does not depend on the availability pattern is an additive function of the availability indicators, so no such layer can represent an interaction between two modalities being jointly present or jointly absent. We propose CAMOS, which gives each modality a bank of second-order oscillators coupled through a matrix that sits inside the differential equation and is gated by availability, so the transition operator itself becomes a function of which measurements were taken. Coupling invalidates the analysis of uncoupled oscillatory models, and we restore it: a per-channel Gershgorin budget makes the effective stiffness positive definite uniformly over all $2^M$ availability patterns and all gaps, an energy argument charges amplification to availability transitions rather than sequence length, and a channel factorization preserves exact associative parallel scans. On ADNI, CAMOS outperforms uncoupled oscillatory state-space models and clinical fusion models on same-visit staging, landmark prediction and longitudinal forecasting, and under zero-shot transfer to OASIS-3 it is the only model that avoids collapse to the majority class.

</details>


### [402] [Mitigating Representation Gaps in Amortized Bayesian Inference with Auxiliary Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39525)
*Hans Olischläger,Svenja Jedhoff,Šimon Kucharský,Aayush Mishra,Stefan T. Radev,Paul Bürkner*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种通用的辅助引导损失方法，通过向内部表示施加监督来改善摊销贝叶斯推断的训练动态，缓解表示瓶颈导致的局部最优问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摊销贝叶斯推断虽可对难以处理的统计模型进行近即时推断，但实际训练中受限于模拟、算力与时间预算，往往只能"满足式"收敛，最优解仍存在可避免的表示鸿沟，通常需要针对具体问题的修补。

Method: 提出对网络内部表示施加辅助引导损失（auxiliary guidance losses）的通用方案；将表示鸿沟形式化为特征学习部分与条件分布学习部分之间信息瓶颈处的局部最优；并提出一种通用诊断手段以区分摘要失败与推断失败。

Result: 训练数据充足时收敛更快，数据稀缺时性能更好；在一系列具有挑战性的真实推断问题上，辅助监督提升了收敛速度与精度。

Conclusion: 辅助引导损失是一种通用且有效的改进摊销推断训练的方法，有助于缓解表示瓶颈造成的局部最优问题。

Abstract: Casting Bayesian inference as a neural network optimization problem targeting an amortized posterior is attractive, as it extends to otherwise intractable statistical models and offers near instantaneous inference for new datasets after prepaying the training cost. Although theory guarantees faithfulness under ideal convergence, practical amortized inference still requires iterating over architectures and optimization choices and ultimately ``satisficing'' under finite simulation, compute, and time budgets. Even the best-performing solution may thus retain avoidable representation gaps that typically require problem-specific fixes. Here, we propose a generic alternative which improves training dynamics with auxiliary guidance losses applied to internal representations. Specifically, we show how such guidance leads to faster convergence when training data is abundant and to better performance when it is scarce. We formalize representation gaps as getting stuck in a local optimum at the information bottleneck between the parts of the network tasked with feature learning and those tasked with conditional distribution learning, and offer a generic diagnostic to separate summary failures from inference failures. Finally, we demonstrate that auxiliary supervision improves convergence speed and accuracy on a range of challenging real-world inference problems.

</details>


### [403] [Estimation of the Label-Noise Transition Matrix with Performance Guarantees via Selective Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39829)
*Xabier de Juan,Santiago Mazuelas,Yilun Zhu,Clayton Scott*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于单侧选择性分类的标签噪声转移矩阵估计方法，绕过类后验估计，提供有限样本性能保证，并给出有效实现算法及其精细的有限样本误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模数据标注成本高昂，带噪标签学习普遍存在，准确的标签噪声转移矩阵估计至关重要。然而现有估计器依赖脆弱的类后验估计，且缺乏有限样本性能保证。

Method: 提出基于单侧选择性分类（one-sided selective classification）的转移矩阵估计方法：规避类后验估计，利用灵活的二元分类学习方法来估计转移矩阵，并设计相应的有效实现算法。

Result: 方法绕过类后验估计，可提供有限样本性能保证，并利用灵活的二元分类学习器；同时给出了所提算法的精细有限样本性能界。

Conclusion: 该工作为标签噪声转移矩阵估计提供了一种不依赖类后验估计、具备有限样本理论保证的新范式，并配有可实现的算法与理论界。

Abstract: Modern machine learning depends heavily on massive datasets, but obtaining high-quality annotations at scale is often expensive. As a result, learning from noisily-labeled data has become common, making accurate estimation of the label-noise transition matrix crucial. However, existing transition matrix estimators rely on the fragile estimation of class-posteriors and do not provide finite-sample performance guarantees. In this work, we propose a novel methodology to estimate the transition matrix based on one-sided selective classification. This approach bypasses class-posterior estimation, provides finite-sample performance guarantees, and leverages flexible learning methods for binary classification. Moreover, we introduce effective algorithms to implement the proposed methodology and provide their refined finite-sample performance bounds.

</details>


### [404] [BayesNDE: Bayesian Generative Modeling for Neural Density Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39843)
*Chenglin Li,Qiao Liu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Abstract: Density estimation is a fundamental problem in statistics and machine learning. In this work, we introduce BayesNDE, a neural density estimator based on Bayesian generative modeling. BayesNDE learns a Bayesian generative model and evaluates its density without requiring invertible networks or Jacobian-determinant computation. For each observation, it infers a sample-specific latent posterior to construct an adaptive proposal that focuses computation on regions contributing most to its density. Bridge sampling then combines samples from this proposal with separate posterior samples to estimate the density. Experiments on nonlinear and multimodal synthetic datasets show improved estimation of density values and better recovery of the density structure compared to the state-of-the-art neural density estimators. Applications to real-world datasets further demonstrate improved anomaly detection. Together, these results highlight BayesNDE as a flexible and effective neural density estimator, demonstrating how posterior inference can turn generative models into tools for density estimation. The code and tutorials are available at https://github.com/liuq-lab/BayesNDE.

</details>


### [405] [Amortized Bayesian Inference on Multilevel Models of Arbitrary Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40024)
*Daniel Habermann,Andreas Bulling,Stefan T. Radev,Paul-Christian Bürkner*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Abstract: We develop a general method for amortized Bayesian inference on multilevel models of arbitrary structure. Given a generative model specified as a directed acyclic graph, our method automatically derives valid factorizations of the joint posterior and matching neural network architectures. The key steps, graph expansion and graph inversion, yield an inverse graph that determines how inference networks are stacked and conditioned, producing factorizations that amortize over the number of groups and the number of observations within each group. Unlike approaches that simplify the dependency structure to speed up learning or inference, our method preserves all conditional independence and exchangeability assumptions of the generative model. Across three case studies, it closely matches gold-standard samplers on models with more than 6,500 parameters while reducing inference to a near-instant forward pass once trained.

</details>


### [406] [Proximal Balancing for Causal Effect Estimation under Unmeasured Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40051)
*Yonghan Jung*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Estimating causal effects from observational data is central to science and policy, but the effects are not identified when confounders are unmeasured. Proximal causal inference addresses this problem with proxies of the unmeasured confounders. However, existing proxy-based approaches either designate proxy roles and solve an inverse problem, which is ill-posed and hard to estimate with high-dimensional proxies, or use a latent-variable model, which assumes that the learned latent variable matches the hidden confounder and leaves bias when it does not. To address these challenges, we introduce proximal balancing. It carries the classical idea of covariate balancing to confounders that are observed only through proxies: it learns a low-dimensional summary of the covariates and proxies that makes the treatment groups comparable, and then adjusts for this summary. It needs no designated proxy roles, inverse problem, or latent model. We give identification theory, finite-sample guarantees, and a practical algorithm, PROBE. We demonstrate the method on low-dimensional, high-dimensional, and image proxies and on real-world data.

</details>


### [407] [Signal Processing over Product DAGs: Causal Shifts and Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40275)
*Sundeep Prabhakar Chepuri,Antonio G. Marques,Maulik Devmurari,Gonzalo Mateos*

Abstract: We develop a signal processing framework for signals indexed by the product of two directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) and described by a linear structural equation model (SEM). Such a setup arises whenever (linear) causal relations act along two domains, as in component versus manufacturing stage or gene versus experimental condition. Disregarding the factorization of the underlying graph and the native two-axis causal structure requires inverting a weighted transitive closure matrix whose size is the product of the two factor sizes for Fourier analysis. Recognizing that standard graph products fail to yield factorizable transitive closures, we introduce a new DAG product under which separability holds. We motivate the new operator in the vertex domain, and show that it also renders the SEM, the Fourier modes, the causal shifts, and the filters on the product DAG separable across its constituent graph factors.

</details>
